# 支付模块实施计划（P3 第 1 项）

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 新建 `ccloud-module-pay` 服务（端口 28085），交付与渠道无关的支付中心：支付应用、渠道配置、支付单、退款单、转账单、钱包与充值；渠道实现 Mock 与钱包；内部服务经 outbox 消息、外部系统经开放平台接入。

**Architecture:** pay 的结构与 open 一致（`pay-api` 放错误码、枚举、Feign 接口与消息体，`pay-biz` 为可执行应用）。渠道层放在 `pay-biz` 的 `framework/pay/core/client` 包：`PayClient` 统一接口，`PayClientProvider` 按渠道编码注册实现，`PayClientFactory` 按渠道编号缓存实例、配置变化时重建。单据状态一律用条件更新流转，状态变更与 outbox 消息同一事务提交；渠道调用放在事务之外。pay 订阅自己发出的消息，完成钱包充值入账、充值退款结果处理与开放平台回调转发。

**Tech Stack:** JDK 21、Spring Boot 3.5.16、Spring Cloud OpenFeign、MyBatis Plus 3.5.17、MySQL 8.4、Redis 8（Redis Stream 作消息通道）、XXL-Job 3.4.2；Mock 渠道的回调用 JDK 自带的 `java.net.http.HttpClient` 发送，不新增依赖。测试用 JUnit 5 + AssertJ + Mockito + MockMvc，连接本机已有的 MySQL（`ccloud_test` 库）与 Redis；回调接收方用 JDK 自带的 `com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer` 模拟。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-pay-design.md`

## Global Constraints

每个任务的验收条件都隐含包含本节。

- 分支 `feat/p3-pay`，只在该分支提交，不推送。
- 构建离线：`mvn -o`。本期不新增需要联网下载的依赖。执行 Maven 前先 `export PATH=$PATH:/Users/yifei/maven/apache-maven-3.9.9/bin`。单模块测试加 `-am`，例如 `mvn -o -q -pl ccloud-module-pay/ccloud-module-pay-biz -am test -Dtest=XxxTest -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`。安静模式下测试通过时没有输出，结果看 `target/surefire-reports/TEST-*.xml`。
- 路径前缀写在 Controller 上：管理端 `/admin-api/pay/**`，会员端 `/app-api/pay/**`，开放接口 `/open-api/pay/**`，内部 `/rpc-api/pay/**`（由 `pay-api` 的 Feign 接口定义）。渠道回调 `/app-api/pay/notify/{order|refund|transfer}/{channelId}` 与 Mock 确认页 `/app-api/pay/mock/confirm` 免登录、免租户头。
- 管理端接口用 `@PreAuthorize("@ss.hasPermission('...')")`，权限标识取自 `sql/mysql/dml/02-menu.sql`（225–256 已有，Task 1 删除 236、239、243、244、245，新增 495）。
- DO 的注解与 open 的 `OpenAppDO` 一致（`@TableName`、`@Data`、`@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)`、`@ToString(callSuper = true)`、`@Accessors(chain = true)`，主键 `@TableId`）。pay 的全部表都按租户隔离，DO 继承 `TenantBaseDO`。不手工赋值 `tenantId`。
- 金额一律 `Integer`，单位分。外部单号由 `PayNoRedisDAO` 生成。
- 状态流转一律条件更新（`WHERE id = ? AND status = 原状态`），影响 0 行即已被他人处理，按幂等返回，不抛异常。渠道调用不放在数据库事务内（钱包渠道的本地事务除外）。渠道调用抛异常时只记 warn 日志，单据保持等待，由补查任务确定结果。
- 业务消息一律经 `OutboxMessageService.saveAndPublish` 在业务事务内写入；消费方用 `IdempotentConsumer.consumeOnce` 幂等。
- Web 集成测试不回滚事务，数据会提交到共享的测试库：测试数据一律带可识别的前缀（应用标识 `webtest-`、商户单号 `WT-`），并在 `@AfterEach` 中删除。
- 错误码在 pay 的 `ErrorCodeConstants` 定义，并在 `sql/mysql/dml/06-i18n.sql` 登记中英文文案；改了 `dml/` 或 `ddl/` 后执行 `scripts/build-sql.sh` 重新生成 `sql/mysql/ccloud.sql`。
- 先写测试再实现；每个任务结束前，对关键逻辑做变异检查：改坏代码，确认有测试失败，再还原。还原后必须 `touch` 被还原的文件，否则 Maven 会沿用改坏时编译出的 class。
- 提交信息用中文 + Conventional Commits 前缀，不加署名行。与用户沟通一律用中文。
- 测试与校验脚本连接本机已有的中间件（见 `LocalMySql` / `LocalRedis`、`scripts/lib/local-middleware.sh`），不临时启动容器，不安装新的中间件；不得删除其他库、不得 FLUSHALL、不得停止或删除任何容器。
- 不执行 `scripts/docker-up.sh --reset-db`：重建本机 `ccloud` 库须先征得用户同意（由控制方处理）。
- 构建镜像后清理本项目的悬空镜像与匿名卷：`docker-up.sh` / `docker-down.sh` 已自动清理；绕开这两个脚本手工 `docker build` / `docker compose` 后，执行 `docker image prune -f --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ccloud` 与 `docker volume prune -f --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ccloud`。只按 ccloud 标签清理。
- 端口：pay 28085；XXL-Job 调度中心保持 48180。
- `ccloud.pay.mock-enabled` 只在 `application-local.yaml`、`application-dev.yaml` 与测试配置中设为 true；不得写进 `application.yaml` 与 Nacos 配置包。
- 全局 gitignore 会忽略 `README.md`、`.gitignore` 与 `log/` 目录：新建这类文件后用 `git status --ignored` 确认已纳入提交。

## Review Focus

以下输入在设计中隐含、最容易出问题，每条都在对应任务里写了专门的测试：

1. 同一支付尝试的渠道回调与补查同时到达 → 支付尝试与订单只成功一次，只写一条 `pay.order.paid`（Task 8：`并发通知同一笔支付只入账一次`）。
2. 渠道回调的金额与订单金额不一致 → 拒绝，单据状态不变，不写消息（Task 8：`回调金额与订单不一致时拒绝且不改状态`；Task 10 退款同理）。
3. 订单已超时关闭后用户才付款成功 → 支付尝试记成功，订单保持已关闭，出现在异常支付列表（Task 8：`订单已关闭后支付成功记为异常支付`）。
4. 同一订单并发发起多笔退款，合计超过支付金额 → 预占额度保证合计不超额（Task 10：`并发退款合计不超过支付金额`）。
5. 开放平台回调转发后消费事务提交失败、消息重投 → 开放平台按事件编号去重，第三方只收到一次（Task 2：`相同事件编号重复发送只记一条回调`；Task 13：`重投时以消息编号作为事件编号…`）。

```java
WX_APP("wx_app", "微信 App 支付", false), WX_NATIVE("wx_native", "微信扫码支付", false),

// transfer/PayTransferStatusEnum.java：WAITING(0, "等待转账")、PROCESSING(5, "转账进行中")、SUCCESS(10, "转账成功")、FAILURE(20, "转账失败")；DICT_TYPE = "pay_transfer_status"
// wallet/PayWalletBizTypeEnum.java：RECHARGE(1, "充值")、RECHARGE_REFUND(2, "充值退款")、PAYMENT(3, "支付")、PAYMENT_REFUND(4, "支付退款")、
```

各状态枚举字段为 `status`（`Integer`）与 `name`，实现 `IntArrayValuable`，写法照 `{oapi}/enums/notify/OpenNotifyStatusEnum.java`；钱包业务类型枚举字段为 `type` 与 `name`。

```java
@GetMapping(PREFIX + "/get")      CommonResult<PayOrderRespDTO> getOrder(@RequestParam("id") Long id);

// {papi}/api/refund/PayRefundApi.java，PREFIX = ApiConstants.PREFIX + "/refund"

// {papi}/api/transfer/PayTransferApi.java，PREFIX = ApiConstants.PREFIX + "/transfer"
@PostMapping(PREFIX + "/create")  CommonResult<Long> createTransfer(@Valid @RequestBody PayTransferCreateReqDTO reqDTO);
@GetMapping(PREFIX + "/get")      CommonResult<PayTransferRespDTO> getTransfer(@RequestParam("id") Long id);

// {papi}/api/wallet/PayWalletApi.java，PREFIX = ApiConstants.PREFIX + "/wallet"
@GetMapping(PREFIX + "/get-balance") CommonResult<Integer> getWalletBalance(@RequestParam("userId") Long userId,
                                                                            @RequestParam("userType") Integer userType);
```

**渠道层**（Task 4 定义，包 `{pbiz}/framework/pay/core/client`）：

```java
// dto/PayClientStatus.java：WAITING（处理中或结果未知）、SUCCESS、FAILURE（渠道明确失败：支付失败或关闭、退款失败、转账失败）
// dto/PayDisplayMode.java：QR_CODE、URL、FORM、APP、NONE

// dto/PayOrderUnifiedReq.java（@Data @Accessors(chain = true)）
String outTradeNo; String subject; String body; Integer price; LocalDateTime expireTime; String notifyUrl;
String userIp; String payMethod; Long userId; Integer userType; Map<String, String> channelExtras;
// dto/PayOrderResult.java
PayClientStatus status; String outTradeNo; String channelOrderNo; String channelUserId; Integer price;
LocalDateTime successTime; PayDisplayMode displayMode; String displayContent; String errorCode; String errorMsg; String rawData;
// dto/PayRefundUnifiedReq.java
String outTradeNo; String outRefundNo; String reason; Integer payPrice; Integer refundPrice; String notifyUrl;
// dto/PayRefundResult.java
PayClientStatus status; String outRefundNo; String channelRefundNo; Integer refundPrice; LocalDateTime successTime;
String errorCode; String errorMsg; String rawData;
// dto/PayTransferUnifiedReq.java
String outTransferNo; String subject; Integer price; String userAccount; String userName; String notifyUrl;
// dto/PayTransferResult.java
PayClientStatus status; String outTransferNo; String channelTransferNo; Integer price; LocalDateTime successTime;
String errorCode; String errorMsg; String rawData;
// dto/PayNotifyRequest.java（@Data @AllArgsConstructor）：Map<String, String> params; Map<String, String> headers（键为小写）; String body;

// PayNotifyVerifyException.java：extends RuntimeException，渠道回调验签或解析失败时抛出，回调入口据此返回 400

// PayClient.java
public interface PayClient {
    Long getChannelId();
    PayOrderResult unifiedOrder(PayOrderUnifiedReq req);
    PayOrderResult getOrder(String outTradeNo);
    PayOrderResult parseOrderNotify(PayNotifyRequest request);
    PayRefundResult unifiedRefund(PayRefundUnifiedReq req);
    PayRefundResult getRefund(String outTradeNo, String outRefundNo);
    PayRefundResult parseRefundNotify(PayNotifyRequest request);
    PayTransferResult unifiedTransfer(PayTransferUnifiedReq req);
    PayTransferResult getTransfer(String outTransferNo);
    PayTransferResult parseTransferNotify(PayNotifyRequest request);
    /** 回调处理成功后应答渠道的响应体 */
    default String notifyAckBody() { return "success"; }
}

// PayClientConfig.java
public interface PayClientConfig {
    /** 配置不合法时抛 IllegalArgumentException，message 作为提示 */
    void validate();
    /** 敏感字段（JSON 属性名），后台回显为 *** */
    default Set<String> sensitiveFields() { return Set.of(); }
}

// PayClientProvider.java —— 每个渠道实现一个，声明为 Spring Bean 即注册
public interface PayClientProvider {
    String getCode();
    Class<? extends PayClientConfig> getConfigClass();
    PayClient create(Long channelId, PayClientConfig config);
}

// PayClientFactory.java（@Component）
public boolean isSupported(String code);
public PayClientConfig parseConfig(String code, String configJson);   // 未实现抛 CHANNEL_NOT_SUPPORTED；解析或校验失败抛 CHANNEL_CONFIG_INVALID
public PayClient getPayClient(Long channelId, String code, String configJson);  // 按编号缓存，编码或配置变化时重建
public void invalidate(Long channelId);
public String maskConfig(String code, String configJson);              // 敏感字段替换为 ***
public String mergeMaskedConfig(String code, String newJson, String oldJson);  // 新配置中值为 *** 的敏感字段取旧值
```

**配置**（Task 3，`{pbiz}/framework/config/PayProperties.java`）：

```java
@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "ccloud.pay") @Component @Validated @Data
public class PayProperties {
    /** 模拟支付开关。只在 local、dev 的 profile 中打开；关闭时不能新建或启用 Mock 渠道，确认页返回 404 */
    private boolean mockEnabled = false;
    /** 渠道回调地址前缀（网关地址），回调地址为 {notifyBaseUrl}/app-api/pay/notify/{order|refund|transfer}/{channelId} */
    @NotBlank private String notifyBaseUrl = "http://127.0.0.1:28080";
    private Wallet wallet = new Wallet();
    private Job job = new Job();

    @Data public static class Wallet {
        /** 钱包充值所用支付应用的标识，各租户自建；默认与演示数据一致 */
        @NotBlank private String rechargeAppKey = "wallet";
        /** 充值支付单的有效期 */
        @NotNull private Duration rechargeExpire = Duration.ofHours(2);
    }

    @Data public static class Job {
        /** 定时任务每次处理的条数上限 */
        @Min(1) private int batchSize = 100;
        /** 单据创建多久后仍在等待才主动查询 */
        @NotNull private Duration syncDelay = Duration.ofMinutes(1);
        /** 只补查这段时间内创建的支付尝试，更早的交给超时关单 */
        @NotNull private Duration syncWindow = Duration.ofDays(1);
    }
}
```

### Task 2: 开放平台回调支持调用方指定事件编号，登记支付事件

**Files:**
- Modify: `{oapi}/api/notify/dto/OpenNotifySendReqDTO.java`、`{oapi}/enums/notify/OpenNotifyEvents.java`
- Modify: `{obiz}/service/notify/OpenNotifyService.java`、`OpenNotifyServiceImpl.java`、`{obiz}/api/notify/OpenNotifyApiImpl.java`、`{obiz}/dal/mysql/notify/OpenNotifyLogMapper.java`
- Test: `{otest}/service/notify/OpenNotifyServiceImplTest.java`、`{otest}/api/notify/OpenNotifyApiImplTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `OpenNotifySendReqDTO.eventId`（可选）；`OpenNotifyService.sendNotify(Long appId, String eventId, String event, Map<String, Object> data)`，`eventId` 为空时自动生成，已存在时返回已有回调日志编号；`OpenNotifyEvents.PAY_ORDER_PAID` 等 5 个常量。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

在 `OpenNotifyServiceImplTest` 中（该类已有创建应用的辅助方法，沿用）新增：

```java
    @Test
    void 相同事件编号重复发送只记一条回调() {
        Long appId = createAppWithNotifyUrl();   // 沿用本类已有的建应用方法；没有则照本类其他用例插入一条带回调地址的应用
        Long first = openNotifyService.sendNotify(appId, "pay-msg-0001", "pay.order.paid", Map.of("merchantOrderId", "M1"));
        Long second = openNotifyService.sendNotify(appId, "pay-msg-0001", "pay.order.paid", Map.of("merchantOrderId", "M1"));

        assertThat(second).isEqualTo(first);
        assertThat(openNotifyLogMapper.selectCount(OpenNotifyLogDO::getEventId, "pay-msg-0001")).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void 未指定事件编号时自动生成() {
        Long appId = createAppWithNotifyUrl();
        Long id = openNotifyService.sendNotify(appId, null, "open.test", Map.of("message", "x"));
        assertThat(openNotifyLogMapper.selectById(id).getEventId()).hasSize(32);
    }
```

在 `OpenNotifyApiImplTest` 新增：事件编号含非法字符（如 `"a b"`）时返回参数错误 400；传入合法编号时透传给服务（`verify(openNotifyService).sendNotify(1L, "pay-msg-0002", "pay.order.paid", data)`，写法照该类现有用例）。

Expected: FAIL（编译失败：方法签名不存在）。

- [ ] **Step 2: 实现**

`OpenNotifySendReqDTO` 增加：

```java
    @Schema(description = "事件编号，供调用方去重：相同编号重复发送只记一条回调。为空时自动生成", example = "5f0c2a9e1b7d4c3e8a6f9b2d1c0e7a54")
    @Pattern(regexp = "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$", message = "事件编号只能包含字母、数字、下划线与中划线，最长 64 位")
    private String eventId;
```

`OpenNotifyEvents` 增加：

```java
    /** 支付成功，由 pay 发送，data 为 merchantOrderId、orderNo、price、status、successTime、channelCode */
    String PAY_ORDER_PAID = "pay.order.paid";
    /** 退款成功 */
    String PAY_REFUND_SUCCESS = "pay.refund.success";
    /** 退款失败 */
    String PAY_REFUND_FAILURE = "pay.refund.failure";
    /** 转账成功 */

```

`OpenNotifyLogMapper` 增加：

```java
    default OpenNotifyLogDO selectByEventId(String eventId) {
        return selectOne(OpenNotifyLogDO::getEventId, eventId);
    }
```

`OpenNotifyService.sendNotify` 签名改为 `Long sendNotify(Long appId, String eventId, String event, Map<String, Object> data)`，注释补"eventId 为空时自动生成；已存在时直接返回已有回调日志编号"。`OpenNotifyServiceImpl.sendNotify` 改为：

```java
    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public Long sendNotify(Long appId, String eventId, String event, Map<String, Object> data) {
        if (StrUtil.isNotBlank(eventId)) {
            // 调用方重投同一事件（如消费事务提交失败后消息重投）时不重复回调
            OpenNotifyLogDO existing = TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> openNotifyLogMapper.selectByEventId(eventId));
            if (existing != null) {
                return existing.getId();
            }
        }
        OpenAppDO app = openAppService.getAppIgnoreTenant(appId);
        if (app == null) {
            throw exception(OPEN_APP_NOT_EXISTS);
        }
        String finalEventId = StrUtil.isNotBlank(eventId) ? eventId : IdUtil.fastSimpleUUID();
        // ……原有组装 body、notifyLog 的代码不变，eventId 处改用 finalEventId……
        try {
            TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> openNotifyLogMapper.insert(notifyLog));
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            // 并发重投：另一线程已写入同一事件
            return TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> openNotifyLogMapper.selectByEventId(finalEventId)).getId();
        }
        if (!noUrl) {
            executeAfterCommit(notifyLog.getId());
        }
        return notifyLog.getId();
    }
```

`sendTestNotify` 内部调用改为 `sendNotify(appId, null, OpenNotifyEvents.TEST, ...)`；`OpenNotifyApiImpl` 改为 `openNotifyService.sendNotify(reqDTO.getAppId(), reqDTO.getEventId(), reqDTO.getEvent(), reqDTO.getData())`。`grep -rn "sendNotify(" ccloud-module-open` 确认没有遗漏的调用点（含测试）。

```java
class EnumDictConsistencyTest {

    private static <E extends Enum<E>> Set<String> values(Class<E> type, Function<E, Object> getter) {
        return Arrays.stream(type.getEnumConstants()).map(getter).map(String::valueOf)
                .collect(Collectors.toCollection(TreeSet::new));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("渠道编码与字典 pay_channel_code 一致")
    void channelCode() {
        assertThat(values(PayChannelEnum.class, PayChannelEnum::getCode))
                .isEqualTo(new TreeSet<>(SqlFixture.dictValues(PayChannelEnum.DICT_TYPE)));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("支付单状态与字典 pay_order_status 一致")

                .isEqualTo(new TreeSet<>(SqlFixture.dictValues(PayOrderStatusEnum.DICT_TYPE)));

                .isEqualTo(new TreeSet<>(SqlFixture.dictValues(PayRefundStatusEnum.DICT_TYPE)));

}
```

按「共享约定」逐字编写错误码、常量、五个枚举、四个 Feign 接口（`@FeignClient(name = ApiConstants.NAME, contextId = "payOrderApi")` 等，`@Tag(name = "RPC 服务 - 支付单")` 等）、DTO、三个消息体。DTO 每个字段加 `@Schema` 描述与示例，写法照 `{oapi}/api/notify/dto/OpenNotifySendReqDTO.java`。消息体示例：

```java
package com.ccloud.module.pay.mq.message;

import lombok.Data;
import lombok.experimental.Accessors;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 支付单消息。支付成功时由 pay 在同一事务内写入 outbox，订阅方按 appKey 过滤自己的单据，并用消息编号幂等
 */
@Data
@Accessors(chain = true)
public class PayOrderMessage {

    public static final String TOPIC_PAID = "pay.order.paid";

    /** 单据所属租户，消费线程据此切换租户上下文 */
    private Long tenantId;
    private Long appId;
    private String appKey;
    private Long orderId;
    /** 支付成功的外部订单号 */
    private String orderNo;
    private String merchantOrderId;
    /** 支付金额，单位分 */
    private Integer price;
    private String channelCode;
    private String payMethod;
    private LocalDateTime successTime;
    private Long userId;
    private Integer userType;
}
```

`PayChannelEnum`：

```java
@Getter
@AllArgsConstructor
public enum PayChannelEnum {

    MOCK("mock", "模拟支付", true),
    WALLET("wallet", "钱包支付", true),
    WX_LITE("wx_lite", "微信小程序支付", false),
    WX_PUB("wx_pub", "微信公众号支付", false),
    WX_APP("wx_app", "微信 App 支付", false),
    WX_NATIVE("wx_native", "微信扫码支付", false),
    ALIPAY_PC("alipay_pc", "支付宝 PC 网站", false),
    ALIPAY_WAP("alipay_wap", "支付宝 Wap 网站", false),
    ALIPAY_APP("alipay_app", "支付宝 App", false),
    ALIPAY_QR("alipay_qr", "支付宝扫码", false);

    public static final String DICT_TYPE = "pay_channel_code";

    private final String code;
    private final String name;
    /** 本期是否有渠道实现。未实现的编码保留在字典中，新建渠道时提示未实现 */
    private final boolean implemented;

    public static PayChannelEnum getByCode(String code) {
        return Arrays.stream(values()).filter(e -> e.code.equals(code)).findFirst().orElse(null);
    }
}
```

`{pbiz}/framework/rpc/config/RpcConfiguration.java`：

```java
package com.ccloud.module.pay.framework.rpc.config;

import com.ccloud.module.open.api.app.OpenAppApi;
import com.ccloud.module.open.api.notify.OpenNotifyApi;
import com.ccloud.module.system.api.dict.DictDataApi;
import com.ccloud.module.system.api.i18n.I18nMessageApi;
import com.ccloud.module.system.api.logger.OperateLogApi;
import com.ccloud.module.system.api.permission.PermissionApi;
import com.ccloud.module.system.api.tenant.TenantApi;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.EnableFeignClients;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * pay 调用其他服务的 Feign 客户端，逐个显式列出。
 * pay-api 自身的接口不注册为客户端：本服务内由本地 RestController 实现
 */
@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
@EnableFeignClients(clients = {
        // 框架契约：权限、租户、字典、国际化、操作日志
        PermissionApi.class, TenantApi.class, DictDataApi.class, I18nMessageApi.class, OperateLogApi.class,
        // 开放平台：绑定开放应用时校验归属；向外部系统发送支付回调
        OpenAppApi.class, OpenNotifyApi.class
})
public class RpcConfiguration {
}
```

`{pbiz}/framework/config/PayProperties.java`：按「共享约定」逐字编写。

`{pbiz}/framework/security/config/SecurityConfiguration.java`（写法照 infra 的同名类）：

```java
/**
 * pay 模块的免鉴权路径。网关侧有独立白名单，两边都要配
 */
@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
public class SecurityConfiguration {

    /**
     * 可匿名访问的接口（不限方法）：渠道回调靠渠道验签保证安全；Mock 确认页只在开启模拟支付的环境可用。
     * 网关的 ccloud.security.ignore-urls 须与此一致，由 GatewayWhitelistConsistencyTest 校验
     */
    public static final String[] ANONYMOUS_URLS = {
            "/app-api/pay/notify/**",
            "/app-api/pay/mock/confirm"
    };

    public static final String[] ANONYMOUS_GET_URLS = {};

    @Bean("payAuthorizeRequestsCustomizer")
    public AuthorizeRequestsCustomizer authorizeRequestsCustomizer() {
        return new AuthorizeRequestsCustomizer() {
            @Override
            public void customize(AuthorizeHttpRequestsConfigurer<HttpSecurity>
                                          .AuthorizationManagerRequestMatcherRegistry registry) {
                registry.requestMatchers(ANONYMOUS_URLS).permitAll();
            }
        };
    }
}
```

网关 `application.yaml` 的 `ignore-urls` 在 `# ---- infra ----` 段之后加：

```yaml
      # ---- pay ----
      - /app-api/pay/notify/**
      - /app-api/pay/mock/confirm
```

`{ptest}/framework/pay/core/client/PayClientFactoryTest.java`（纯单元测试，不起 Spring）：

```java
class PayClientFactoryTest {

    /** 测试用渠道：记录创建次数，配置 key 不能为空，secret 为敏感字段 */
    static class CountingProvider implements PayClientProvider {
        final AtomicInteger created = new AtomicInteger();
        final String code;
        CountingProvider(String code) { this.code = code; }
        @Override public String getCode() { return code; }
        @Override public Class<? extends PayClientConfig> getConfigClass() { return TestConfig.class; }
        @Override public PayClient create(Long channelId, PayClientConfig config) {
            created.incrementAndGet();
            FakePayClient client = new FakePayClient();
            client.channelId = channelId;
            return client;
        }
    }

    @Data
    public static class TestConfig implements PayClientConfig {
        private String key;
        private String secret;
        @Override public void validate() { if (StrUtil.isBlank(key)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("key 不能为空"); }
        @Override public Set<String> sensitiveFields() { return Set.of("secret"); }
    }

    private final CountingProvider provider = new CountingProvider("mock");
    private final PayClientFactory factory = new PayClientFactory(List.of(provider));

    @Test
    void 按编号缓存_配置不变时返回同一实例() {
        PayClient a = factory.getPayClient(1L, "mock", "{\"key\":\"a\"}");
        PayClient b = factory.getPayClient(1L, "mock", "{\"key\":\"a\"}");
        assertThat(b).isSameAs(a);
        assertThat(provider.created).hasValue(1);
    }

    @Test
    void 配置变化时重建() {
        PayClient a = factory.getPayClient(1L, "mock", "{\"key\":\"a\"}");
        PayClient b = factory.getPayClient(1L, "mock", "{\"key\":\"b\"}");
        assertThat(b).isNotSameAs(a);
        assertThat(provider.created).hasValue(2);
    }

    @Test
    void 未实现的编码抛渠道未实现() {
        assertThat(factory.isSupported("wx_lite")).isFalse();
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> factory.parseConfig("wx_lite", "{}"))
                .isInstanceOf(ServiceException.class).extracting("code").isEqualTo(CHANNEL_NOT_SUPPORTED.getCode());
    }

    @Test
    void 配置校验失败抛配置不正确且带原因() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> factory.parseConfig("mock", "{\"key\":\"\"}"))
                .isInstanceOf(ServiceException.class)
                .satisfies(e -> {
                    assertThat(((ServiceException) e).getCode()).isEqualTo(CHANNEL_CONFIG_INVALID.getCode());
                    assertThat(e.getMessage()).contains("key 不能为空");
                });
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> factory.parseConfig("mock", "not-json"))
                .isInstanceOf(ServiceException.class).extracting("code").isEqualTo(CHANNEL_CONFIG_INVALID.getCode());
    }

    @Test
    void 重复注册同一编码启动失败() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new PayClientFactory(List.of(provider, new CountingProvider("mock"))))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalStateException.class).hasMessageContaining("mock");
    }

    @Test
    void 敏感字段回显为掩码_保存掩码时保留原值() {
        String masked = factory.maskConfig("mock", "{\"key\":\"a\",\"secret\":\"s3cr3t\"}");
        assertThat(masked).contains("\"secret\":\"***\"").contains("\"key\":\"a\"").doesNotContain("s3cr3t");

        String merged = factory.mergeMaskedConfig("mock", "{\"key\":\"b\",\"secret\":\"***\"}", "{\"key\":\"a\",\"secret\":\"s3cr3t\"}");
        assertThat(merged).contains("\"secret\":\"s3cr3t\"").contains("\"key\":\"b\"");

        String replaced = factory.mergeMaskedConfig("mock", "{\"key\":\"b\",\"secret\":\"new\"}", "{\"key\":\"a\",\"secret\":\"s3cr3t\"}");
        assertThat(replaced).contains("\"secret\":\"new\"");
    }
}
```

```java
@Import(PayNoRedisDAO.class)
class PayNoRedisDAOTest extends BasePayDbAndRedisUnitTest {

    @Resource
    private PayNoRedisDAO payNoRedisDAO;

    @Test
    void 单号为前缀加时间加六位序号() {
        assertThat(payNoRedisDAO.generate(PayNoRedisDAO.ORDER_PREFIX)).matches("^P\\d{14}\\d{6}$");
    }

    @Test
    void 并发生成不重复() throws Exception {
        Set<String> nos = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(8);
        List<Future<?>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            futures.add(pool.submit(() -> { for (int j = 0; j < 50; j++) nos.add(payNoRedisDAO.generate("R")); }));
        }
        for (Future<?> f : futures) f.get();
        pool.shutdown();
        assertThat(nos).hasSize(400);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 写 DTO 与接口**

按「共享约定·渠道层」编写 `PayClient`、`PayClientConfig`、`PayClientProvider`、`PayNotifyVerifyException`、两个枚举与 7 个 DTO（`@Data @Accessors(chain = true)`，`PayNotifyRequest` 用 `@Data @AllArgsConstructor`）。结果类的静态工厂示例（另两个同构）：

```java
@Data
@Accessors(chain = true)
public class PayOrderResult {

    private PayClientStatus status;
    private String outTradeNo;
    private String channelOrderNo;
    /** 渠道用户编号，如微信 openid */
    private String channelUserId;
    /** 渠道确认的金额，单位分。成功时必填，用于与订单金额核对 */
    private Integer price;
    private LocalDateTime successTime;
    /** 等待用户支付时的展示方式与内容 */
    private PayDisplayMode displayMode;
    private String displayContent;
    private String errorCode;
    private String errorMsg;
    /** 渠道原始报文，存入 channel_notify_data 供对账排查 */
    private String rawData;

    public static PayOrderResult waiting(String outTradeNo, PayDisplayMode mode, String content) {
        return new PayOrderResult().setStatus(PayClientStatus.WAITING).setOutTradeNo(outTradeNo)
                .setDisplayMode(mode).setDisplayContent(content);
    }

    public static PayOrderResult success(String outTradeNo, String channelOrderNo, Integer price,
                                         LocalDateTime successTime, String rawData) {
        return new PayOrderResult().setStatus(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS).setOutTradeNo(outTradeNo)
                .setChannelOrderNo(channelOrderNo).setPrice(price).setSuccessTime(successTime)
                .setDisplayMode(PayDisplayMode.NONE).setRawData(rawData);
    }

    public static PayOrderResult failure(String outTradeNo, String errorCode, String errorMsg, String rawData) {
        return new PayOrderResult().setStatus(PayClientStatus.FAILURE).setOutTradeNo(outTradeNo)
                .setErrorCode(errorCode).setErrorMsg(errorMsg).setRawData(rawData);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 写工厂**

```java
package com.ccloud.module.pay.framework.pay.core.client;

/**
 * 渠道客户端工厂：按渠道编号缓存客户端；每次取用都带上库中的编码与配置，二者变化时重建。
 * 多实例部署时各实例在下一次取用时自行发现配置变化，无需广播
 */
@Component
public class PayClientFactory {

    public static final String MASK = "***";

    private final Map<String, PayClientProvider> providers;
    private final Map<Long, CachedClient> clients = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public PayClientFactory(List<PayClientProvider> providerList) {
        Map<String, PayClientProvider> map = new HashMap<>();
        for (PayClientProvider provider : providerList) {
            if (map.putIfAbsent(provider.getCode(), provider) != null) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("渠道编码重复注册：" + provider.getCode());
            }
        }
        this.providers = Map.copyOf(map);
    }

    public boolean isSupported(String code) {
        return providers.containsKey(code);
    }

    public PayClientConfig parseConfig(String code, String configJson) {
        PayClientProvider provider = getProvider(code);
        PayClientConfig config;
        try {
            config = JsonUtils.parseObject(StrUtil.blankToDefault(configJson, "{}"), provider.getConfigClass());
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_CONFIG_INVALID, "不是合法的 JSON");
        }
        if (config == null) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_CONFIG_INVALID, "不能为空");
        }
        try {
            config.validate();
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_CONFIG_INVALID, e.getMessage());
        }
        return config;
    }

    public PayClient getPayClient(Long channelId, String code, String configJson) {
        return clients.compute(channelId, (id, cached) -> {
            if (cached != null && cached.code().equals(code) && cached.configJson().equals(configJson)) {
                return cached;
            }
            return new CachedClient(code, configJson, getProvider(code).create(id, parseConfig(code, configJson)));
        }).client();
    }

    public void invalidate(Long channelId) {
        clients.remove(channelId);
    }

    public String maskConfig(String code, String configJson) {
        Map<String, Object> map = toMap(configJson);
        for (String field : sensitiveFields(code)) {
            if (map.get(field) != null) {
                map.put(field, MASK);
            }
        }
        return JsonUtils.toJsonString(map);
    }

    public String mergeMaskedConfig(String code, String newJson, String oldJson) {
        Map<String, Object> merged = toMap(newJson);
        Map<String, Object> old = toMap(oldJson);
        for (String field : sensitiveFields(code)) {
            if (MASK.equals(merged.get(field))) {
                merged.put(field, old.get(field));
            }
        }
        return JsonUtils.toJsonString(merged);
    }

    private Set<String> sensitiveFields(String code) {
        return BeanUtils.instantiateClass(getProvider(code).getConfigClass()).sensitiveFields();
    }

    private static Map<String, Object> toMap(String json) {
        if (StrUtil.isBlank(json)) {
            return new LinkedHashMap<>();
        }
        try {
            Map<String, Object> map = JsonUtils.parseObject(json, new TypeReference<LinkedHashMap<String, Object>>() {});
            return map == null ? new LinkedHashMap<>() : map;
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_CONFIG_INVALID, "不是合法的 JSON");
        }
    }

    private PayClientProvider getProvider(String code) {
        PayClientProvider provider = providers.get(code);
        if (provider == null) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_NOT_SUPPORTED, code);
        }
        return provider;
    }

    private record CachedClient(String code, String configJson, PayClient client) {
    }
}
```

（`BeanUtils` 为 `org.springframework.beans.BeanUtils`；`JsonUtils.parseObject` 在解析失败时抛异常还是返回 null 以 `JsonUtils` 源码为准，两种情况都已处理。）

- [ ] **Step 4: 写单号生成**

```java
package com.ccloud.module.pay.dal.redis.no;

/**
 * 外部单号生成：前缀 + 秒级时间 + 当秒自增序号。单号须全局唯一（各表 uk_no 不分租户），
 * 因此直接用 StringRedisTemplate，不经按租户加前缀的缓存
 */
@Repository
public class PayNoRedisDAO {

    public static final String ORDER_PREFIX = "P";
    public static final String REFUND_PREFIX = "R";
    public static final String TRANSFER_PREFIX = "T";
    public static final String WALLET_TRANSACTION_PREFIX = "W";
    public static final String WALLET_ADJUST_PREFIX = "A";

    private static final String KEY_FORMAT = "pay:no:%s";
    private static final DateTimeFormatter TIME = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMddHHmmss");

    @Resource
    private StringRedisTemplate stringRedisTemplate;

    public String generate(String prefix) {
        String time = LocalDateTime.now().format(TIME);
        String key = String.format(KEY_FORMAT, prefix + time);
        Long seq = stringRedisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key);
        if (seq != null && seq == 1L) {
            stringRedisTemplate.expire(key, Duration.ofMinutes(1));
        }
        return prefix + time + String.format("%06d", seq);
    }
}
```

`{ptest}/support/FakePayClient.java`：

```java
/**
 * 测试用渠道客户端：各方法的返回值由测试预先设置，未设置时返回等待。字段均为 public，便于测试直接改
 */
public class FakePayClient implements PayClient {

    public volatile Long channelId;
    public volatile Function<PayOrderUnifiedReq, PayOrderResult> onUnifiedOrder;
    public volatile Function<String, PayOrderResult> onGetOrder;
    public volatile Function<PayRefundUnifiedReq, PayRefundResult> onUnifiedRefund;
    public volatile Function<String, PayRefundResult> onGetRefund;
    public volatile Function<PayTransferUnifiedReq, PayTransferResult> onUnifiedTransfer;
    public volatile Function<String, PayTransferResult> onGetTransfer;
    /** 非空时每次调用都抛出它，模拟渠道超时等异常 */
    public volatile RuntimeException throwOnCall;
    public volatile PayOrderUnifiedReq lastOrderReq;
    public volatile PayRefundUnifiedReq lastRefundReq;
    public volatile PayTransferUnifiedReq lastTransferReq;
    public final AtomicInteger unifiedOrderCalls = new AtomicInteger();
    public final AtomicInteger unifiedTransferCalls = new AtomicInteger();

    public void reset() {
        onUnifiedOrder = null; onGetOrder = null; onUnifiedRefund = null; onGetRefund = null;
        onUnifiedTransfer = null; onGetTransfer = null; throwOnCall = null;
        lastOrderReq = null; lastRefundReq = null; lastTransferReq = null;
        unifiedOrderCalls.set(0); unifiedTransferCalls.set(0);
    }

    private void maybeThrow() {
        if (throwOnCall != null) throw throwOnCall;
    }

    @Override public Long getChannelId() { return channelId; }

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult unifiedOrder(PayOrderUnifiedReq req) {
        lastOrderReq = req; unifiedOrderCalls.incrementAndGet(); maybeThrow();
        return onUnifiedOrder != null ? onUnifiedOrder.apply(req)
                : PayOrderResult.waiting(req.getOutTradeNo(), PayDisplayMode.URL, "https://fake.pay/" + req.getOutTradeNo());
    }

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult getOrder(String outTradeNo) {
        maybeThrow();
        return onGetOrder != null ? onGetOrder.apply(outTradeNo) : PayOrderResult.waiting(outTradeNo, PayDisplayMode.NONE, null);
    }

    /** 回调报文即 PayOrderResult 的 JSON；请求头 x-fake-sign 为 bad 时验签失败 */
    @Override
    public PayOrderResult parseOrderNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        if ("bad".equals(request.getHeaders().get("x-fake-sign"))) throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("验签失败");
        return JsonUtils.parseObject(request.getBody(), PayOrderResult.class);
    }

    @Override
    public PayRefundResult unifiedRefund(PayRefundUnifiedReq req) {
        lastRefundReq = req; maybeThrow();
        return onUnifiedRefund != null ? onUnifiedRefund.apply(req) : PayRefundResult.waiting(req.getOutRefundNo());
    }

    @Override
    public PayRefundResult getRefund(String outTradeNo, String outRefundNo) {
        maybeThrow();
        return onGetRefund != null ? onGetRefund.apply(outRefundNo) : PayRefundResult.waiting(outRefundNo);
    }

    @Override
    public PayRefundResult parseRefundNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        if ("bad".equals(request.getHeaders().get("x-fake-sign"))) throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("验签失败");
        return JsonUtils.parseObject(request.getBody(), PayRefundResult.class);
    }

    @Override
    public PayTransferResult unifiedTransfer(PayTransferUnifiedReq req) {
        lastTransferReq = req; unifiedTransferCalls.incrementAndGet(); maybeThrow();
        return onUnifiedTransfer != null ? onUnifiedTransfer.apply(req) : PayTransferResult.waiting(req.getOutTransferNo());
    }

    @Override
    public PayTransferResult getTransfer(String outTransferNo) {
        maybeThrow();
        return onGetTransfer != null ? onGetTransfer.apply(outTransferNo) : PayTransferResult.waiting(outTransferNo);
    }

    @Override
    public PayTransferResult parseTransferNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        if ("bad".equals(request.getHeaders().get("x-fake-sign"))) throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("验签失败");
        return JsonUtils.parseObject(request.getBody(), PayTransferResult.class);
    }
}
```

`PayAppMapper`：

```java
@Mapper
public interface PayAppMapper extends BaseMapperX<PayAppDO> {

    default PageResult<PayAppDO> selectPage(PayAppPageReqVO reqVO) {
        return selectPage(reqVO, new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayAppDO>()
                .likeIfPresent(PayAppDO::getName, reqVO.getName())
                .eqIfPresent(PayAppDO::getAppKey, reqVO.getAppKey())
                .eqIfPresent(PayAppDO::getStatus, reqVO.getStatus())
                .orderByDesc(PayAppDO::getId));
    }

    default PayAppDO selectByAppKey(String appKey) {
        return selectOne(PayAppDO::getAppKey, appKey);
    }

    default PayAppDO selectByOpenAppId(Long openAppId) {
        return selectOne(PayAppDO::getOpenAppId, openAppId);
    }
}
```

`PayChannelMapper`：`selectByAppIdAndCode(Long appId, String code)`、`selectListByAppId(Long appId)`、`selectListByAppIdAndStatus(Long appId, Integer status)`，写法同上。

```java
// PayAppSaveReqVO
Long id;                                                     // 更新时必填
@NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{1,63}$", message = "应用标识只能包含小写字母、数字、下划线与中划线，2 到 64 位")
String appKey;                                               // 创建后不可修改，更新时忽略
@NotBlank @Size(max = 64) String name;
@NotNull @InEnum(CommonStatusEnum.class) Integer status;
@Size(max = 500) String remark;
@Size(max = 64) String openAppKey;                            // 绑定的开放应用标识；为空表示不绑定

// PayAppPageReqVO extends PageParam：String name; String appKey; Integer status;
// PayAppRespVO：Long id; String appKey; String name; Integer status; String remark; Long openAppId; LocalDateTime createTime;

// PayChannelSaveReqVO
Long id;
@NotNull Long appId;                                         // 更新时忽略
@NotBlank String code;                                       // 更新时忽略
@NotNull @InEnum(CommonStatusEnum.class) Integer status;
@NotNull @DecimalMin("0") @DecimalMax("100") Double feeRate;
@Size(max = 500) String remark;
@NotBlank @Size(max = 2000, message = "渠道配置不能超过 2000 个字符") String config;   // 加密后须放得进 varchar(4096)

// PayChannelRespVO：Long id; Long appId; String code; Integer status; Double feeRate; String remark; String config（已脱敏）; LocalDateTime createTime;
```

（`@InEnum` 的包名以 open 的 VO 为准。）

```java
@Import({PayAppServiceImpl.class})
class PayAppServiceImplTest extends BasePayDbUnitTest {

    @Resource
    private PayAppServiceImpl payAppService;
    @Resource
    private PayAppMapper payAppMapper;
    @MockitoBean
    private OpenAppApi openAppApi;

    private static PayAppSaveReqVO req(String appKey, String openAppKey) {
        return new PayAppSaveReqVO().setAppKey(appKey).setName("测试应用").setStatus(0).setOpenAppKey(openAppKey);
    }

    private void stubOpenApp(String appKey, long id, long tenantId) {
        OpenAppSnapshotRespDTO snapshot = new OpenAppSnapshotRespDTO();
        snapshot.setId(id);
        snapshot.setAppKey(appKey);
        snapshot.setTenantId(tenantId);
        when(openAppApi.getAppSnapshot(appKey)).thenReturn(CommonResult.success(snapshot));
    }

    @Test
    void 创建应用_标识在租户内唯一() {
        payAppService.createApp(req("mall-test", null));
        assertServiceException(() -> payAppService.createApp(req("mall-test", null)), PAY_APP_KEY_EXISTS);
    }

    @Test
    void 绑定本租户的开放应用() {
        stubOpenApp("open-1", 91L, TENANT_ID);
        Long id = payAppService.createApp(req("mall-test", "open-1"));
        assertThat(payAppMapper.selectById(id).getOpenAppId()).isEqualTo(91L);
        assertThat(payAppService.validPayAppByOpenAppId(91L).getId()).isEqualTo(id);
    }

    @Test
    void 不能绑定其他租户或不存在的开放应用() {
        stubOpenApp("open-2", 92L, 2L);
        when(openAppApi.getAppSnapshot("missing")).thenReturn(CommonResult.success(null));
        assertServiceException(() -> payAppService.createApp(req("a1", "open-2")), PAY_APP_OPEN_APP_INVALID);
        assertServiceException(() -> payAppService.createApp(req("a2", "missing")), PAY_APP_OPEN_APP_INVALID);
    }

    @Test
    void 一个开放应用只能绑定一个支付应用() {
        stubOpenApp("open-1", 91L, TENANT_ID);
        payAppService.createApp(req("a1", "open-1"));
        assertServiceException(() -> payAppService.createApp(req("a2", "open-1")), PAY_APP_OPEN_APP_BOUND);
    }

    @Test
    void 更新时不修改应用标识_可解除绑定() {
        stubOpenApp("open-1", 91L, TENANT_ID);
        Long id = payAppService.createApp(req("a1", "open-1"));
        payAppService.updateApp(req("changed", null).setId(id));
        PayAppDO app = payAppMapper.selectById(id);
        assertThat(app.getAppKey()).isEqualTo("a1");
        assertThat(app.getOpenAppId()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void 未绑定或已禁用时开放应用不能使用支付() {
        assertServiceException(() -> payAppService.validPayAppByOpenAppId(999L), PAY_APP_NOT_OPENED_FOR_OPEN_APP);
        stubOpenApp("open-1", 91L, TENANT_ID);
        payAppService.createApp(req("a1", "open-1").setStatus(1));
        assertServiceException(() -> payAppService.validPayAppByOpenAppId(91L), PAY_APP_IS_DISABLE);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现服务**

`PayAppServiceImpl`（`@Service @Validated`）要点：

```java
    @Override
    public Long createApp(PayAppSaveReqVO reqVO) {
        if (payAppMapper.selectByAppKey(reqVO.getAppKey()) != null) {
            throw exception(PAY_APP_KEY_EXISTS);
        }
        PayAppDO app = new PayAppDO().setAppKey(reqVO.getAppKey()).setName(reqVO.getName())
                .setStatus(reqVO.getStatus()).setRemark(reqVO.getRemark())
                .setOpenAppId(resolveOpenAppId(reqVO.getOpenAppKey(), null));
        payAppMapper.insert(app);
        return app.getId();
    }

    @Override
    public void updateApp(PayAppSaveReqVO reqVO) {
        PayAppDO app = validateAppExists(reqVO.getId());
        // 应用标识被内部服务与钱包充值引用，创建后不可修改
        payAppMapper.updateById(new PayAppDO().setId(app.getId()).setName(reqVO.getName()).setStatus(reqVO.getStatus())
                .setRemark(reqVO.getRemark()).setOpenAppId(resolveOpenAppId(reqVO.getOpenAppKey(), app.getId())));
        if (StrUtil.isBlank(reqVO.getOpenAppKey()) && app.getOpenAppId() != null) {
            // updateById 不更新 null 字段，解除绑定须显式置空
            payAppMapper.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<PayAppDO>().eq(PayAppDO::getId, app.getId())
                    .set(PayAppDO::getOpenAppId, null));
        }
    }

    /**
     * 开放应用须存在且属于当前租户，且未被其他支付应用绑定
     */
    private Long resolveOpenAppId(String openAppKey, Long selfId) {
        if (StrUtil.isBlank(openAppKey)) {
            return null;
        }
        OpenAppSnapshotRespDTO snapshot = openAppApi.getAppSnapshot(openAppKey).getCheckedData();
        if (snapshot == null || !Objects.equals(snapshot.getTenantId(), TenantContextHolder.getRequiredTenantId())) {
            throw exception(PAY_APP_OPEN_APP_INVALID);
        }
        PayAppDO bound = payAppMapper.selectByOpenAppId(snapshot.getId());
        if (bound != null && !Objects.equals(bound.getId(), selfId)) {
            throw exception(PAY_APP_OPEN_APP_BOUND);
        }
        return snapshot.getId();
    }

    @Override
    public PayAppDO validPayAppByOpenAppId(Long openAppId) {
        PayAppDO app = payAppMapper.selectByOpenAppId(openAppId);
        if (app == null) {
            throw exception(PAY_APP_NOT_OPENED_FOR_OPEN_APP);
        }
        if (!CommonStatusEnum.ENABLE.getStatus().equals(app.getStatus())) {
            throw exception(PAY_APP_IS_DISABLE);
        }
        return app;
    }
```

（快照中含开放应用密钥：只取编号与租户，不记录日志、不保存。`TenantContextHolder.getRequiredTenantId()` 的方法名以框架为准。）`validPayApp(Long)`、`validPayApp(String)` 同理（不存在 `PAY_APP_NOT_FOUND`，禁用 `PAY_APP_IS_DISABLE`）；`getAppIgnoreTenant` 用 `TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> payAppMapper.selectById(id))`；`deleteApp` 本任务只做"存在才删"，同时删除该应用的渠道并 `payClientFactory.invalidate`（订单检查在 Task 8 补）。

`PayChannelServiceImpl` 要点：

```java
    @Override
    public Long createChannel(PayChannelSaveReqVO reqVO) {
        if (payAppService.getApp(reqVO.getAppId()) == null) {
            throw exception(PAY_APP_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        validateCodeSupported(reqVO.getCode());
        if (payChannelMapper.selectByAppIdAndCode(reqVO.getAppId(), reqVO.getCode()) != null) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_EXIST_SAME_CHANNEL_ERROR);
        }
        payClientFactory.parseConfig(reqVO.getCode(), reqVO.getConfig());
        PayChannelDO channel = new PayChannelDO().setAppId(reqVO.getAppId()).setCode(reqVO.getCode())
                .setStatus(reqVO.getStatus()).setFeeRate(reqVO.getFeeRate()).setRemark(reqVO.getRemark())
                .setConfig(reqVO.getConfig());
        payChannelMapper.insert(channel);
        return channel.getId();
    }

    @Override
    public void updateChannel(PayChannelSaveReqVO reqVO) {
        PayChannelDO channel = validateChannelExists(reqVO.getId());
        if (CommonStatusEnum.ENABLE.getStatus().equals(reqVO.getStatus())) {
            validateCodeSupported(channel.getCode());
        }
        String config = payClientFactory.isSupported(channel.getCode())
                ? payClientFactory.mergeMaskedConfig(channel.getCode(), reqVO.getConfig(), channel.getConfig())
                : channel.getConfig();
        if (payClientFactory.isSupported(channel.getCode())) {
            payClientFactory.parseConfig(channel.getCode(), config);
        }
        payChannelMapper.updateById(new PayChannelDO().setId(channel.getId()).setStatus(reqVO.getStatus())
                .setFeeRate(reqVO.getFeeRate()).setRemark(reqVO.getRemark()).setConfig(config));
        payClientFactory.invalidate(channel.getId());
    }

    /**
     * Mock 渠道由 ccloud.pay.mock-enabled 控制是否注册：未注册时给出专门的提示，便于定位环境配置
     */
    private void validateCodeSupported(String code) {
        PayChannelEnum channel = PayChannelEnum.getByCode(code);
        if (channel == PayChannelEnum.MOCK && !payClientFactory.isSupported(code)) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_MOCK_DISABLED);
        }
        if (channel == null || !payClientFactory.isSupported(code)) {
            throw exception(CHANNEL_NOT_SUPPORTED, code);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public PayClient getPayClient(PayChannelDO channel) {
        return payClientFactory.getPayClient(channel.getId(), channel.getCode(), channel.getConfig());
    }

    @Override
    public String buildNotifyUrl(String type, Long channelId) {
        return StrUtil.removeSuffix(payProperties.getNotifyBaseUrl(), "/") + "/app-api/pay/notify/" + type + "/" + channelId;
    }
```

`validPayChannel(appId, code)`：`selectByAppIdAndCode` 为空 `CHANNEL_NOT_FOUND`，禁用 `CHANNEL_IS_DISABLE`。`getChannelIgnoreTenant` 用 `TenantUtils.executeIgnore`。`deleteChannel`：存在才删，删后 `invalidate`。

`PayChannelController`（`/admin-api/pay/channel`）：`POST /create`（`pay:channel:create`）、`PUT /update`（`pay:channel:update`）、`DELETE /delete?id=`（`pay:channel:delete`）、`GET /get?id=`、`GET /list?appId=`（`pay:channel:query`）。返回前用 `payClientFactory.maskConfig(code, config)` 脱敏；编码已不受支持（如生产环境里遗留的 mock 渠道）时返回 `{}`，不抛错：

```java
    private PayChannelRespVO toResp(PayChannelDO channel) {
        String config = payClientFactory.isSupported(channel.getCode())
                ? payClientFactory.maskConfig(channel.getCode(), channel.getConfig()) : "{}";
        return BeanUtils.toBean(channel, PayChannelRespVO.class).setConfig(config);
    }
```

（`BeanUtils.toBean` 为 `com.ccloud.framework.common.util.object.BeanUtils`。）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PayNoRedisDAO`（Task 4）、`PayClientProvider`（Task 4）。
- Produces: 共享约定中的 `PayWalletService`；`WalletPayClientProvider`（编码 `wallet`）；`PayWalletApiImpl`。

- [ ] **Step 1: DO 与 Mapper**

`PayWalletDO`（`pay_wallet`）：`Long id; Long userId; Integer userType; Integer balance; Integer totalExpense; Integer totalRecharge; Integer freezePrice;`。`PayWalletTransactionDO`（`pay_wallet_transaction`）：`Long id; String no; Long walletId; Integer bizType; String bizId; String title; Integer price; Integer balance;`。

```java
@Mapper
public interface PayWalletMapper extends BaseMapperX<PayWalletDO> {

    default PayWalletDO selectByUser(Long userId, Integer userType) {
        return selectOne(PayWalletDO::getUserId, userId, PayWalletDO::getUserType, userType);
    }

    default PageResult<PayWalletDO> selectPage(PayWalletPageReqVO reqVO) {
        return selectPage(reqVO, new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayWalletDO>()
                .eqIfPresent(PayWalletDO::getUserId, reqVO.getUserId())
                .eqIfPresent(PayWalletDO::getUserType, reqVO.getUserType())
                .orderByDesc(PayWalletDO::getId));
    }

    /**
     * 原子变更余额与累计值。余额变更后不得为负：条件不满足时影响 0 行（余额不足或钱包不存在）。
     * 禁止先查后改——并发扣款会扣成负数
     */
    @Update("UPDATE pay_wallet SET balance = balance + #{balanceDelta}, total_expense = total_expense + #{expenseDelta}, "
            + "total_recharge = total_recharge + #{rechargeDelta} "
            + "WHERE id = #{id} AND deleted = 0 AND balance + #{balanceDelta} >= 0")
    int updateBalance(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("balanceDelta") int balanceDelta,
                      @Param("expenseDelta") int expenseDelta, @Param("rechargeDelta") int rechargeDelta);
}
```

（多租户拦截器会给该语句加上 `tenant_id` 条件，调用时须处于钱包所属租户的上下文。）

```java
@Mapper
public interface PayWalletTransactionMapper extends BaseMapperX<PayWalletTransactionDO> {

    default PayWalletTransactionDO selectByWalletAndBiz(Long walletId, Integer bizType, String bizId) {
        return selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayWalletTransactionDO>().eq(PayWalletTransactionDO::getWalletId, walletId)
                .eq(PayWalletTransactionDO::getBizType, bizType).eq(PayWalletTransactionDO::getBizId, bizId));
    }

    default PayWalletTransactionDO selectByBiz(Integer bizType, String bizId) {
        return selectOne(PayWalletTransactionDO::getBizType, bizType, PayWalletTransactionDO::getBizId, bizId);
    }

    default PageResult<PayWalletTransactionDO> selectPage(Long walletId, PageParam pageParam) {
        return selectPage(pageParam, new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayWalletTransactionDO>()
                .eq(PayWalletTransactionDO::getWalletId, walletId).orderByDesc(PayWalletTransactionDO::getId));
    }
}
```

```java
@Import({PayWalletServiceImpl.class, PayNoRedisDAO.class})
class PayWalletServiceImplTest extends BasePayDbAndRedisUnitTest {

    @Resource
    private PayWalletServiceImpl walletService;
    @Resource
    private PayWalletMapper walletMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayWalletTransactionMapper transactionMapper;
    @Resource
    private JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    private PayWalletDO walletWithBalance(long userId, int balance) {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(userId, UserTypeEnum.MEMBER.getValue());
        if (balance > 0) {
            walletService.addBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.ADJUST, "init-" + userId, "初始", balance);
        }
        return walletMapper.selectById(wallet.getId());
    }

    @Test
    void 首次访问自动开通钱包_再次访问返回同一个() {
        PayWalletDO a = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(70001L, 1);
        PayWalletDO b = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(70001L, 1);
        assertThat(b.getId()).isEqualTo(a.getId());
        assertThat(a.getBalance()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void 余额不足时不扣成负数且不写流水() {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletWithBalance(70002L, 100);
        assertServiceException(() -> walletService.reduceBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT, "P1", "支付", 101),
                WALLET_BALANCE_NOT_ENOUGH);
        assertThat(walletMapper.selectById(wallet.getId()).getBalance()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(transactionMapper.selectByBiz(PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT.getType(), "P1")).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void 同一业务单据重复入账只记一次() {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletWithBalance(70003L, 0);
        PayWalletTransactionDO a = walletService.addBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.RECHARGE, "WR1", "充值", 500);
        PayWalletTransactionDO b = walletService.addBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.RECHARGE, "WR1", "充值", 500);
        assertThat(b.getId()).isEqualTo(a.getId());
        PayWalletDO after = walletMapper.selectById(wallet.getId());
        assertThat(after.getBalance()).isEqualTo(500);
        assertThat(after.getTotalRecharge()).isEqualTo(500);
    }

    @Test
    void 支付累计支出_支付退款冲减支出_流水记录变更后余额() {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletWithBalance(70004L, 1000);
        PayWalletTransactionDO pay = walletService.reduceBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT, "P2", "支付", 300);
        assertThat(pay.getPrice()).isEqualTo(-300);
        assertThat(pay.getBalance()).isEqualTo(700);
        walletService.addBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT_REFUND, "R2", "退款", 100);
        PayWalletDO after = walletMapper.selectById(wallet.getId());
        assertThat(after.getBalance()).isEqualTo(800);
        assertThat(after.getTotalExpense()).isEqualTo(200);
    }

    @Test
    void 后台调整金额为0报错_减少超过余额报错() {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletWithBalance(70005L, 50);
        assertServiceException(() -> walletService.adjustBalance(wallet.getId(), 0, "x"), WALLET_ADJUST_PRICE_ZERO);
        assertServiceException(() -> walletService.adjustBalance(wallet.getId(), -51, "x"), WALLET_BALANCE_NOT_ENOUGH);
        walletService.adjustBalance(wallet.getId(), -50, "扣回");
        assertThat(walletMapper.selectById(wallet.getId()).getBalance()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NOT_SUPPORTED)
    void 并发扣款只成功到余额为止() throws Exception {
        long userId = 70006L;
        Long walletId = TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, () -> walletWithBalance(userId, 500).getId());
        try {
            ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(10);
            AtomicInteger ok = new AtomicInteger();
            List<Future<?>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
                String bizId = "PC" + i;
                futures.add(pool.submit(() -> TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, () -> {
                    try {
                        walletService.reduceBalance(walletId, PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT, bizId, "支付", 100);
                        ok.incrementAndGet();
                    } catch (ServiceException ignored) {
                        // 余额不足
                    }
                })));
            }
            for (Future<?> f : futures) f.get();
            pool.shutdown();
            assertThat(ok).hasValue(5);
            assertThat(TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, () -> walletMapper.selectById(walletId)).getBalance()).isZero();
        } finally {
            jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_wallet_transaction WHERE wallet_id = ?", walletId);
            jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_wallet WHERE id = ?", walletId);
        }
    }

    @Test
    @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NOT_SUPPORTED)
    void 并发首次访问只开通一个钱包() throws Exception {
        long userId = 70007L;
        try {
            ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(6);
            Set<Long> ids = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
            List<Future<?>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                futures.add(pool.submit(() -> TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID,
                        () -> ids.add(walletService.getOrCreateWallet(userId, 1).getId()))));
            }
            for (Future<?> f : futures) f.get();
            pool.shutdown();
            assertThat(ids).hasSize(1);
        } finally {
            jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_wallet WHERE user_id = ?", userId);
        }
    }
}
```

（`TenantUtils.execute(Long, ...)` 带返回值的重载以框架源码为准。）

```java
@Import({PayWalletServiceImpl.class, PayNoRedisDAO.class, WalletPayClientProvider.class})
class WalletPayClientTest extends BasePayDbAndRedisUnitTest {

    @Resource
    private WalletPayClientProvider provider;
    @Resource
    private PayWalletServiceImpl walletService;

    private WalletPayClient client() {
        return (WalletPayClient) provider.create(1L, new WalletPayClientConfig());
    }

    private PayOrderUnifiedReq orderReq(String no, long userId, int price) {
        return new PayOrderUnifiedReq().setOutTradeNo(no).setSubject("测试").setPrice(price)
                .setUserId(userId).setUserType(UserTypeEnum.MEMBER.getValue());
    }

    @Test
    void 余额足够时直接支付成功_无需用户操作() {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(71001L, 1);
        walletService.addBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.ADJUST, "init", "初始", 1000);
        PayOrderResult result = client().unifiedOrder(orderReq("P71001", 71001L, 300));
        assertThat(result.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
        assertThat(result.getDisplayMode()).isEqualTo(PayDisplayMode.NONE);
        assertThat(result.getPrice()).isEqualTo(300);
        assertThat(client().getOrder("P71001").getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
    }

    @Test
    void 余额不足返回失败结果而不抛异常() {
        PayOrderResult result = client().unifiedOrder(orderReq("P71002", 71002L, 300));
        assertThat(result.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.FAILURE);
        assertThat(result.getErrorMsg()).contains("余额不足");
        assertThat(client().getOrder("P71002").getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.WAITING);
    }

    @Test
    void 未指定会员时返回失败结果() {
        PayOrderResult result = client().unifiedOrder(orderReq("P71003", 0L, 300).setUserId(null));
        assertThat(result.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.FAILURE);
    }

    @Test
    void 退款退回原钱包_重复退款只退一次() {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(71004L, 1);
        walletService.addBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.ADJUST, "init", "初始", 1000);
        client().unifiedOrder(orderReq("P71004", 71004L, 300));
        PayRefundUnifiedReq req = new PayRefundUnifiedReq().setOutTradeNo("P71004").setOutRefundNo("R71004")
                .setRefundPrice(100).setPayPrice(300).setReason("退");
        assertThat(client().unifiedRefund(req).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
        client().unifiedRefund(req);
        assertThat(walletService.getWallet(wallet.getId()).getBalance()).isEqualTo(800);
        assertThat(client().getRefund("P71004", "R71004").getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
        assertThat(client().getRefund("P71004", "R-none").getStatus()).as("未入账返回等待，由补查重发").isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.WAITING);
    }

    @Test
    void 转账入账到收款会员的钱包_收款账号须为会员编号() {
        PayTransferUnifiedReq req = new PayTransferUnifiedReq().setOutTransferNo("T71005").setSubject("佣金")
                .setPrice(200).setUserAccount("71005");
        assertThat(client().unifiedTransfer(req).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
        assertThat(walletService.getOrCreateWallet(71005L, 1).getBalance()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(client().getTransfer("T71005").getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);

        PayTransferUnifiedReq bad = new PayTransferUnifiedReq().setOutTransferNo("T71006").setPrice(1).setUserAccount("abc");
        assertThat(client().unifiedTransfer(bad).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.FAILURE);
    }

    @Test
    void 钱包渠道没有回调() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client().parseOrderNotify(new PayNotifyRequest(Map.of(), Map.of(), "{}")))
                .isInstanceOf(ServiceException.class).extracting("code").isEqualTo(CHANNEL_OPERATION_NOT_SUPPORTED.getCode());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现钱包服务**

```java
@Service
@Validated
@Slf4j
public class PayWalletServiceImpl implements PayWalletService {

    @Resource
    private PayWalletMapper walletMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayWalletTransactionMapper transactionMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayNoRedisDAO payNoRedisDAO;

    @Override
    public PayWalletDO getOrCreateWallet(Long userId, Integer userType) {
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletMapper.selectByUser(userId, userType);
        if (wallet != null) {
            return wallet;
        }
        PayWalletDO created = new PayWalletDO().setUserId(userId).setUserType(userType)
                .setBalance(0).setTotalExpense(0).setTotalRecharge(0).setFreezePrice(0);
        try {
            walletMapper.insert(created);
            return created;
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            // 并发首次访问：另一请求已开通
            return walletMapper.selectByUser(userId, userType);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public PayWalletTransactionDO addBalance(Long walletId, PayWalletBizTypeEnum bizType, String bizId, String title, int price) {
        Assert.isTrue(price > 0, "入账金额须为正数");
        PayWalletTransactionDO existing = transactionMapper.selectByWalletAndBiz(walletId, bizType.getType(), bizId);
        if (existing != null) {
            return existing;
        }
        int expenseDelta = bizType == PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT_REFUND ? -price : 0;
        int rechargeDelta = bizType == PayWalletBizTypeEnum.RECHARGE ? price : 0;
        if (walletMapper.updateBalance(walletId, price, expenseDelta, rechargeDelta) == 0) {
            throw exception(WALLET_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return insertTransaction(walletId, bizType, bizId, title, price);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public PayWalletTransactionDO reduceBalance(Long walletId, PayWalletBizTypeEnum bizType, String bizId, String title, int price) {
        Assert.isTrue(price > 0, "扣减金额须为正数");
        PayWalletTransactionDO existing = transactionMapper.selectByWalletAndBiz(walletId, bizType.getType(), bizId);
        if (existing != null) {
            return existing;
        }
        int expenseDelta = bizType == PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT ? price : 0;
        int rechargeDelta = bizType == PayWalletBizTypeEnum.RECHARGE_REFUND ? -price : 0;
        if (walletMapper.updateBalance(walletId, -price, expenseDelta, rechargeDelta) == 0) {
            throw exception(walletMapper.selectById(walletId) == null ? WALLET_NOT_FOUND : WALLET_BALANCE_NOT_ENOUGH);
        }
        return insertTransaction(walletId, bizType, bizId, title, -price);
    }

    /**
     * 同一 (钱包, 业务类型, 业务编号) 并发写入时唯一约束冲突：异常抛出使本事务连同余额变更一起回滚，
     * 调用方重试时会命中已有流水直接返回
     */
    private PayWalletTransactionDO insertTransaction(Long walletId, PayWalletBizTypeEnum bizType, String bizId,
                                                     String title, int price) {
        PayWalletTransactionDO transaction = new PayWalletTransactionDO()
                .setNo(payNoRedisDAO.generate(PayNoRedisDAO.WALLET_TRANSACTION_PREFIX)).setWalletId(walletId)
                .setBizType(bizType.getType()).setBizId(bizId).setTitle(StrUtil.maxLength(title, 120)).setPrice(price)
                .setBalance(walletMapper.selectById(walletId).getBalance());
        transactionMapper.insert(transaction);
        return transaction;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void adjustBalance(Long walletId, int price, String reason) {
        if (price == 0) {
            throw exception(WALLET_ADJUST_PRICE_ZERO);
        }
        if (walletMapper.selectById(walletId) == null) {
            throw exception(WALLET_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        String bizId = payNoRedisDAO.generate(PayNoRedisDAO.WALLET_ADJUST_PREFIX);
        String title = "后台调整：" + reason;
        if (price > 0) {
            addBalance(walletId, PayWalletBizTypeEnum.ADJUST, bizId, title, price);
        } else {
            reduceBalance(walletId, PayWalletBizTypeEnum.ADJUST, bizId, title, -price);
        }
    }

    // getWallet、getWalletPage、getWalletTransactionPage、getTransaction 直接委托 Mapper
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现钱包渠道**

```java
/**
 * 钱包渠道：站内余额支付。没有回调，查询按流水判断；扣款流水的业务编号即支付尝试的外部单号
 */
public class WalletPayClient implements PayClient {

    private final Long channelId;
    private final PayWalletService walletService;

    public WalletPayClient(Long channelId, PayWalletService walletService) {
        this.channelId = channelId;
        this.walletService = walletService;
    }

    @Override
    public Long getChannelId() {
        return channelId;
    }

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult unifiedOrder(PayOrderUnifiedReq req) {
        if (req.getUserId() == null) {
            return PayOrderResult.failure(req.getOutTradeNo(), "USER_REQUIRED", "钱包支付须指定付款会员", null);
        }
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(req.getUserId(), req.getUserType());
        try {
            PayWalletTransactionDO tx = walletService.reduceBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT,
                    req.getOutTradeNo(), "支付：" + req.getSubject(), req.getPrice());
            return PayOrderResult.success(req.getOutTradeNo(), tx.getNo(), req.getPrice(), LocalDateTime.now(), null);
        } catch (ServiceException e) {
            if (!WALLET_BALANCE_NOT_ENOUGH.getCode().equals(e.getCode())) {
                throw e;
            }
            return PayOrderResult.failure(req.getOutTradeNo(), String.valueOf(e.getCode()), e.getMessage(), null);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult getOrder(String outTradeNo) {
        PayWalletTransactionDO tx = walletService.getTransaction(PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT, outTradeNo);
        return tx == null ? PayOrderResult.waiting(outTradeNo, PayDisplayMode.NONE, null)
                : PayOrderResult.success(outTradeNo, tx.getNo(), -tx.getPrice(), tx.getCreateTime(), null);
    }

    @Override
    public PayRefundResult unifiedRefund(PayRefundUnifiedReq req) {
        PayWalletTransactionDO payment = walletService.getTransaction(PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT, req.getOutTradeNo());
        if (payment == null) {
            return PayRefundResult.failure(req.getOutRefundNo(), "PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND", "未找到钱包支付流水", null);
        }
        PayWalletTransactionDO tx = walletService.addBalance(payment.getWalletId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT_REFUND,
                req.getOutRefundNo(), "退款：" + StrUtil.nullToEmpty(req.getReason()), req.getRefundPrice());
        return PayRefundResult.success(req.getOutRefundNo(), tx.getNo(), req.getRefundPrice(), LocalDateTime.now(), null);
    }

    /** 钱包退款是同步的：没有流水说明发起时未入账（如进程中断），返回等待，由补查任务重发（按退款单号幂等） */
    @Override
    public PayRefundResult getRefund(String outTradeNo, String outRefundNo) {
        PayWalletTransactionDO tx = walletService.getTransaction(PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT_REFUND, outRefundNo);
        return tx == null ? PayRefundResult.waiting(outRefundNo)
                : PayRefundResult.success(outRefundNo, tx.getNo(), tx.getPrice(), tx.getCreateTime(), null);
    }

    @Override
    public PayTransferResult unifiedTransfer(PayTransferUnifiedReq req) {
        Long userId = NumberUtil.isLong(req.getUserAccount()) ? Long.valueOf(req.getUserAccount()) : null;
        if (userId == null) {
            return PayTransferResult.failure(req.getOutTransferNo(), "ACCOUNT_INVALID", "收款账号不是会员编号", null);
        }
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(userId, UserTypeEnum.MEMBER.getValue());
        PayWalletTransactionDO tx = walletService.addBalance(wallet.getId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.TRANSFER_IN,
                req.getOutTransferNo(), "转账：" + StrUtil.nullToEmpty(req.getSubject()), req.getPrice());
        return PayTransferResult.success(req.getOutTransferNo(), tx.getNo(), req.getPrice(), LocalDateTime.now(), null);
    }

    /** 没有流水返回等待，由补查任务重发（按转账单号幂等） */
    @Override
    public PayTransferResult getTransfer(String outTransferNo) {
        PayWalletTransactionDO tx = walletService.getTransaction(PayWalletBizTypeEnum.TRANSFER_IN, outTransferNo);
        return tx == null ? PayTransferResult.waiting(outTransferNo)
                : PayTransferResult.success(outTransferNo, tx.getNo(), tx.getPrice(), tx.getCreateTime(), null);
    }

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult parseOrderNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        throw exception(CHANNEL_OPERATION_NOT_SUPPORTED, PayChannelEnum.WALLET.getCode());
    }

    @Override
    public PayRefundResult parseRefundNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        throw exception(CHANNEL_OPERATION_NOT_SUPPORTED, PayChannelEnum.WALLET.getCode());
    }

    @Override
    public PayTransferResult parseTransferNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        throw exception(CHANNEL_OPERATION_NOT_SUPPORTED, PayChannelEnum.WALLET.getCode());
    }
}
```

`WalletPayClientConfig`：无字段，`validate()` 为空。`WalletPayClientProvider`（`@Component`）：编码 `PayChannelEnum.WALLET.getCode()`，`create` 返回 `new WalletPayClient(channelId, payWalletService)`。

- [ ] **Step 5: 接口**

- `PayWalletApiImpl`（`@RestController @Validated`）：`getWalletBalance` 查 `selectByUser`，不存在返回 0（不为查询开通钱包）。从 `RpcApiCoverageTest.PENDING_APIS` 移除 `PayWalletApi`。
- `PayWalletController`（`/admin-api/pay/wallet`）：`GET /page`、`GET /get?id=`（`pay:wallet:query`）；`PUT /update-balance`（`pay:wallet:update-balance`，请求体 `PayWalletUpdateBalanceReqVO`：`@NotNull Long id; @NotNull Integer price; @NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String reason;`）。
- `PayWalletTransactionController`（`/admin-api/pay/wallet-transaction`）：`GET /page?walletId=`（`pay:wallet:query`）。
- `AppPayWalletController`（`/app-api/pay/wallet`）：`GET /get` 以登录会员（`getLoginUserId()`、`UserTypeEnum.MEMBER`）调用 `getOrCreateWallet`，返回余额、累计支出、累计充值。
- `AppPayWalletTransactionController`（`/app-api/pay/wallet-transaction`）：`GET /page` 查登录会员自己钱包的流水。

Web 测试：
- `PayWalletControllerTest`：经 JDBC 插入会员 `70101` 的钱包（余额 100，`tenant_id` 1），`ADMIN_ID` 调整 `+50` 后 `GET /get` 余额 150，`/admin-api/pay/wallet-transaction/page?walletId=` 有一条"后台调整"流水；`NO_PERM_ADMIN_ID` 调整返回 403。`@AfterEach` 删除该钱包及流水。
- `AppPayWalletControllerTest`：`asMember(get("/app-api/pay/wallet/get"), 70102L)` 返回余额 0 且库中已开通；管理员身份访问返回 401（沿用 member 测试的断言写法）。`@AfterEach` 删除该会员的钱包。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`MockPayNotifySignerTest`：

```java
class MockPayNotifySignerTest {

    private static final String SECRET = "0123456789abcdef";

    @Test
    void 正确签名通过() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        String body = "{\"no\":\"P1\"}";
        String sign = MockPayNotifySigner.sign(SECRET, now, body);
        assertThatCode(() -> MockPayNotifySigner.verify(SECRET, headers(now, sign), body, now)).doesNotThrowAnyException();
    }

    @Test
    void 篡改报文_错误密钥_时间戳偏差超过5分钟_缺少签名头都拒绝() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        String body = "{\"no\":\"P1\"}";
        String sign = MockPayNotifySigner.sign(SECRET, now, body);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> MockPayNotifySigner.verify(SECRET, headers(now, sign), "{\"no\":\"P2\"}", now))
                .isInstanceOf(PayNotifyVerifyException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> MockPayNotifySigner.verify("another-secret-16", headers(now, sign), body, now))
                .isInstanceOf(PayNotifyVerifyException.class);
        long old = now - Duration.ofMinutes(6).toMillis();
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> MockPayNotifySigner.verify(SECRET, headers(old, MockPayNotifySigner.sign(SECRET, old, body)), body, now))
                .isInstanceOf(PayNotifyVerifyException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> MockPayNotifySigner.verify(SECRET, Map.of(), body, now))
                .isInstanceOf(PayNotifyVerifyException.class);
    }

    private static Map<String, String> headers(long ts, String sign) {
        return Map.of("x-mock-timestamp", String.valueOf(ts), "x-mock-sign", sign);
    }
}
```

`MockPayClientConfigTest`：密钥为空或短于 16 位、`delaySeconds` 不在 1–60、`behavior` 为空，`validate()` 均抛 `IllegalArgumentException`；`sensitiveFields()` 为 `Set.of("notifySecret")`。

`MockPayClientTest`（继承 `BasePayDbAndRedisUnitTest`，只用 Redis；回调接收方用 `HttpServer`）：

```java
class MockPayClientTest extends BasePayDbAndRedisUnitTest {

    private static final String SECRET = "0123456789abcdef";

    @Resource
    private StringRedisTemplate stringRedisTemplate;

    private HttpServer server;
    private final BlockingQueue<Map<String, String>> received = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>();
    private MockPayNotifySender sender;
    private String notifyUrl;

    @BeforeEach
    void startReceiver() throws IOException {
        server = HttpServer.create(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", 0), 0);
        server.createContext("/notify", exchange -> {
            String body = new String(exchange.getRequestBody().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            received.add(Map.of("body", body,
                    "ts", exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("X-Mock-Timestamp"),
                    "sign", exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("X-Mock-Sign")));
            exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, 0);
            exchange.close();
        });
        server.start();
        notifyUrl = "http://127.0.0.1:" + server.getAddress().getPort() + "/notify";
        sender = new MockPayNotifySender();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void stop() {
        server.stop(0);
        sender.destroy();
    }

    private MockPayClient client(MockPayBehavior behavior) {
        MockPayClientConfig config = new MockPayClientConfig().setNotifySecret(SECRET).setBehavior(behavior).setDelaySeconds(1);
        return new MockPayClient(1L, config, stringRedisTemplate, sender);
    }

    private PayOrderUnifiedReq order(String no) {
        return new PayOrderUnifiedReq().setOutTradeNo(no).setSubject("测试").setPrice(100).setNotifyUrl(notifyUrl);
    }

    private static String uniqueNo(String prefix) {
        return prefix + IdUtil.fastSimpleUUID().substring(0, 12);
    }

    private static PayNotifyRequest asRequest(Map<String, String> got) {
        return new PayNotifyRequest(Map.of(), Map.of("x-mock-timestamp", got.get("ts"), "x-mock-sign", got.get("sign")), got.get("body"));
    }

    @Test
    void 下单返回确认页地址_确认前查单为等待() {
        String no = uniqueNo("P");
        PayOrderResult result = client(MockPayBehavior.SUCCESS).unifiedOrder(order(no));
        assertThat(result.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.WAITING);
        assertThat(result.getDisplayMode()).isEqualTo(PayDisplayMode.URL);
        assertThat(result.getDisplayContent()).isEqualTo("/app-api/pay/mock/confirm?no=" + no);
        assertThat(client(MockPayBehavior.SUCCESS).getOrder(no).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.WAITING);
    }

    @Test
    void 确认后回调成功结果且签名可验证_再次确认不重复回调() throws Exception {
        String no = uniqueNo("P");
        MockPayClient client = client(MockPayBehavior.SUCCESS);
        client.unifiedOrder(order(no));
        client.confirm(no);
        client.confirm(no);

        Map<String, String> got = received.poll(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertThat(got).isNotNull();
        PayOrderResult parsed = client.parseOrderNotify(asRequest(got));
        assertThat(parsed.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
        assertThat(parsed.getOutTradeNo()).isEqualTo(no);
        assertThat(parsed.getPrice()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(client.getOrder(no).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
        assertThat(received.poll(1500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)).as("重复确认不应再次回调").isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void 丢弃回调时不回调_但查单为成功() throws Exception {
        String no = uniqueNo("P");
        MockPayClient client = client(MockPayBehavior.DROP_NOTIFY);
        client.unifiedOrder(order(no));
        client.confirm(no);
        assertThat(received.poll(1500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)).isNull();
        assertThat(client.getOrder(no).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
    }

    @Test
    void 支付失败时回调失败结果() throws Exception {
        String no = uniqueNo("P");
        MockPayClient client = client(MockPayBehavior.FAIL);
        client.unifiedOrder(order(no));
        client.confirm(no);
        Map<String, String> got = received.poll(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertThat(got).isNotNull();
        assertThat(got.get("body")).contains("\"status\":\"FAILURE\"");
        assertThat(client.getOrder(no).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.FAILURE);
    }

    @Test
    void 延迟回调在配置的秒数之后到达() throws Exception {
        String no = uniqueNo("P");
        MockPayClient client = client(MockPayBehavior.DELAY);
        client.unifiedOrder(order(no));
        long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
        client.confirm(no);
        assertThat(received.poll(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isNotNull();
        assertThat(System.currentTimeMillis() - start).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(900);
    }

    @Test
    void 退款与转账发起后异步回调_查询结果一致_重复发起不重复回调() throws Exception {
        MockPayClient client = client(MockPayBehavior.SUCCESS);
        String refundNo = uniqueNo("R");
        PayRefundUnifiedReq refundReq = new PayRefundUnifiedReq().setOutTradeNo("P1").setOutRefundNo(refundNo)
                .setRefundPrice(30).setPayPrice(100).setNotifyUrl(notifyUrl);
        assertThat(client.unifiedRefund(refundReq).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.WAITING);
        Map<String, String> got = received.poll(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertThat(got.get("body")).contains("\"type\":\"refund\"").contains(refundNo);
        assertThat(client.parseRefundNotify(asRequest(got)).getRefundPrice()).isEqualTo(30);
        assertThat(client.getRefund("P1", refundNo).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
        client.unifiedRefund(refundReq);
        assertThat(received.poll(1500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)).as("重复发起同一退款不再回调").isNull();

        String transferNo = uniqueNo("T");
        client.unifiedTransfer(new PayTransferUnifiedReq().setOutTransferNo(transferNo).setPrice(20).setUserAccount("a").setNotifyUrl(notifyUrl));
        assertThat(received.poll(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS).get("body")).contains("\"type\":\"transfer\"");
        assertThat(client.getTransfer(transferNo).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayClientStatus.SUCCESS);
    }

    @Test
    void 回调类型不符时拒绝() throws Exception {
        MockPayClient client = client(MockPayBehavior.SUCCESS);
        String refundNo = uniqueNo("R");
        client.unifiedRefund(new PayRefundUnifiedReq().setOutTradeNo("P1").setOutRefundNo(refundNo).setRefundPrice(1).setNotifyUrl(notifyUrl));
        Map<String, String> got = received.poll(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.parseOrderNotify(asRequest(got))).isInstanceOf(PayNotifyVerifyException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void 确认不存在的支付返回false() {
        assertThat(client(MockPayBehavior.SUCCESS).confirm("P-not-exists")).isFalse();
    }
}
```
