- [ ] **Step 2: 实现**

`MockPayBehavior`：`SUCCESS`（成功并立即回调）、`FAIL`（支付失败并回调）、`DROP_NOTIFY`（成功但不回调，验证补查）、`DELAY`（成功并在 `delaySeconds` 秒后回调）。

`MockPayClientConfig`（`@Data @Accessors(chain = true)`）：

```java
public class MockPayClientConfig implements PayClientConfig {

    /** 回调签名密钥，至少 16 位 */
    private String notifySecret;
    private MockPayBehavior behavior = MockPayBehavior.SUCCESS;
    /** DELAY 行为的延迟秒数 */
    private Integer delaySeconds = 3;

    @Override
    public void validate() {
        if (StrUtil.length(notifySecret) < 16) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("notifySecret 至少 16 位");
        }
        if (behavior == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("behavior 不能为空");
        }
        if (delaySeconds == null || delaySeconds < 1 || delaySeconds > 60) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("delaySeconds 须在 1 到 60 之间");
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Set<String> sensitiveFields() {
        return Set.of("notifySecret");
    }
}
```

`MockPayNotifySigner`：

```java
public final class MockPayNotifySigner {

    public static final String HEADER_TIMESTAMP = "X-Mock-Timestamp";
    public static final String HEADER_SIGN = "X-Mock-Sign";
    private static final long MAX_SKEW_MILLIS = Duration.ofMinutes(5).toMillis();

    private MockPayNotifySigner() {
    }

    public static String sign(String secret, long timestamp, String body) {
        HMac mac = new HMac(HmacAlgorithm.HmacSHA256, secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        return Base64.encode(mac.digest(timestamp + "\n" + body));
    }

    /**
     * @param headers 键为小写
     */
    public static void verify(String secret, Map<String, String> headers, String body, long now) {
        String ts = headers.get(HEADER_TIMESTAMP.toLowerCase());
        String sign = headers.get(HEADER_SIGN.toLowerCase());
        if (!NumberUtil.isLong(ts) || StrUtil.isBlank(sign)) {
            throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("缺少签名头");
        }
        long timestamp = Long.parseLong(ts);
        if (Math.abs(now - timestamp) > MAX_SKEW_MILLIS) {
            throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("时间戳超出允许范围");
        }
        byte[] expected = sign(secret, timestamp, StrUtil.nullToEmpty(body)).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        if (!MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, sign.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
            throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("签名不正确");
        }
    }
}
```

（`HMac`、`HmacAlgorithm`、`Base64`、`NumberUtil`、`StrUtil` 为 Hutool。）

`MockPayState`（Redis 中保存的模拟单据，`@Data @Accessors(chain = true)`）：`String type; String no; String outTradeNo; Integer price; String notifyUrl; String status;`（`WAITING`/`SUCCESS`/`FAILURE`）`; Long successTime;`。

`MockPayNotifySender`（`@Component @ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "ccloud.pay", name = "mock-enabled", havingValue = "true")`，实现 `DisposableBean`；测试中直接 `new`）：

```java
@Slf4j
public class MockPayNotifySender implements DisposableBean {

    private final HttpClient httpClient = HttpClient.newBuilder().connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
            .followRedirects(HttpClient.Redirect.NEVER).build();
    private final ScheduledExecutorService executor = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(r -> {
        Thread thread = new Thread(r, "pay-mock-notify");
        thread.setDaemon(true);
        return thread;
    });

    /**
     * 异步发送回调，失败只记日志：真实渠道的回调同样可能丢失，由补查任务兜底
     */
    public void send(String url, String secret, String body, long delaySeconds) {
        executor.schedule(() -> {
            long ts = System.currentTimeMillis();
            HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create(url)).timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                    .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .header(MockPayNotifySigner.HEADER_TIMESTAMP, String.valueOf(ts))
                    .header(MockPayNotifySigner.HEADER_SIGN, MockPayNotifySigner.sign(secret, ts, body))
                    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(body)).build();
            try {
                HttpResponse<String> response = httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
                log.info("[mock-notify] {} 返回 {}：{}", url, response.statusCode(), StrUtil.maxLength(response.body(), 200));
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[mock-notify] 回调 {} 失败，等待补查", url, e);
            }
        }, delaySeconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }

    @Override
    public void destroy() {
        executor.shutdownNow();
    }
}
```

`MockPayClient`：

```java
/**
 * 模拟渠道：单据状态存 Redis（多实例查单一致），按配置的行为模拟成功、失败、丢回调、延迟回调。
 * 支付须用户访问确认页（confirm）；退款与转账发起即按行为处理
 */
public class MockPayClient implements PayClient {

    static final String CONFIRM_PATH = "/app-api/pay/mock/confirm?no=";
    private static final String KEY_FORMAT = "pay:mock:%s:%s";
    private static final Duration TTL = Duration.ofDays(1);

    private final Long channelId;
    private final MockPayClientConfig config;
    private final StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;
    private final MockPayNotifySender sender;

    // 构造器按字段顺序赋值；getChannelId 返回 channelId

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult unifiedOrder(PayOrderUnifiedReq req) {
        save(new MockPayState().setType("order").setNo(req.getOutTradeNo()).setPrice(req.getPrice())
                .setNotifyUrl(req.getNotifyUrl()).setStatus("WAITING"));
        return PayOrderResult.waiting(req.getOutTradeNo(), PayDisplayMode.URL, CONFIRM_PATH + req.getOutTradeNo());
    }

    /**
     * 用户在确认页点击支付
     *
     * @return 单据不存在（如已过期）返回 false
     */
    public boolean confirm(String outTradeNo) {
        MockPayState state = load("order", outTradeNo);
        if (state == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if ("WAITING".equals(state.getStatus())) {
            finish(state);
        }
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult getOrder(String outTradeNo) {
        MockPayState state = load("order", outTradeNo);
        if (state == null || "WAITING".equals(state.getStatus())) {
            return PayOrderResult.waiting(outTradeNo, PayDisplayMode.URL, CONFIRM_PATH + outTradeNo);
        }
        return toOrderResult(state, JsonUtils.toJsonString(state));
    }

    @Override
    public PayOrderResult parseOrderNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        return toOrderResult(verifyAndParse(request, "order"), request.getBody());
    }

    /**
     * 按退款单号幂等：已发起过时返回当前结果，不重复回调（补查任务会对等待中的退款重发）
     */
    @Override
    public PayRefundResult unifiedRefund(PayRefundUnifiedReq req) {
        MockPayState existing = load("refund", req.getOutRefundNo());
        if (existing != null) {
            return "WAITING".equals(existing.getStatus()) ? PayRefundResult.waiting(req.getOutRefundNo())
                    : toRefundResult(existing, JsonUtils.toJsonString(existing));
        }
        MockPayState state = new MockPayState().setType("refund").setNo(req.getOutRefundNo()).setOutTradeNo(req.getOutTradeNo())
                .setPrice(req.getRefundPrice()).setNotifyUrl(req.getNotifyUrl()).setStatus("WAITING");
        save(state);
        finish(state);
        return PayRefundResult.waiting(req.getOutRefundNo());
    }

    @Override
    public PayRefundResult getRefund(String outTradeNo, String outRefundNo) {
        MockPayState state = load("refund", outRefundNo);
        if (state == null || "WAITING".equals(state.getStatus())) {
            return PayRefundResult.waiting(outRefundNo);
        }
        return toRefundResult(state, JsonUtils.toJsonString(state));
    }

    @Override
    public PayRefundResult parseRefundNotify(PayNotifyRequest request) {
        return toRefundResult(verifyAndParse(request, "refund"), request.getBody());
    }

    // unifiedTransfer（同样按转账单号幂等）、getTransfer、parseTransferNotify 与退款同构，type 为 transfer，
    // 结果用 toTransferResult（成功：PayTransferResult.success(no, "MOCK-" + no, price, 时间, raw)；失败：failure(no, "MOCK_FAIL", "模拟转账失败", raw)）

    /**
     * 按行为落定结果并决定是否、何时回调
     */
    private void finish(MockPayState state) {
        boolean fail = config.getBehavior() == MockPayBehavior.FAIL;
        state.setStatus(fail ? "FAILURE" : "SUCCESS").setSuccessTime(fail ? null : System.currentTimeMillis());
        save(state);
        if (config.getBehavior() == MockPayBehavior.DROP_NOTIFY || StrUtil.isBlank(state.getNotifyUrl())) {
            return;
        }
        long delay = config.getBehavior() == MockPayBehavior.DELAY ? config.getDelaySeconds() : 0;
        Map<String, Object> body = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        body.put("type", state.getType());
        body.put("no", state.getNo());
        body.put("status", state.getStatus());
        body.put("channelNo", "MOCK-" + state.getNo());
        body.put("price", state.getPrice());
        body.put("successTime", state.getSuccessTime());
        sender.send(state.getNotifyUrl(), config.getNotifySecret(), JsonUtils.toJsonString(body), delay);
    }

    private MockPayState verifyAndParse(PayNotifyRequest request, String expectedType) {
        MockPayNotifySigner.verify(config.getNotifySecret(), request.getHeaders(), request.getBody(), System.currentTimeMillis());
        Map<String, Object> body;
        try {
            body = JsonUtils.parseObject(request.getBody(), new TypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("报文不是合法的 JSON");
        }
        if (body == null || !expectedType.equals(body.get("type"))) {
            throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("回调类型不符");
        }
        return new MockPayState().setType(expectedType).setNo(String.valueOf(body.get("no")))
                .setStatus(String.valueOf(body.get("status")))
                .setPrice(body.get("price") == null ? null : Integer.valueOf(String.valueOf(body.get("price"))))
                .setSuccessTime(body.get("successTime") == null ? null : Long.valueOf(String.valueOf(body.get("successTime"))));
    }

    private PayOrderResult toOrderResult(MockPayState state, String raw) {
        if ("SUCCESS".equals(state.getStatus())) {
            return PayOrderResult.success(state.getNo(), "MOCK-" + state.getNo(), state.getPrice(), toTime(state.getSuccessTime()), raw);
        }
        return PayOrderResult.failure(state.getNo(), "MOCK_FAIL", "模拟支付失败", raw);
    }

    private PayRefundResult toRefundResult(MockPayState state, String raw) {
        if ("SUCCESS".equals(state.getStatus())) {
            return PayRefundResult.success(state.getNo(), "MOCK-" + state.getNo(), state.getPrice(), toTime(state.getSuccessTime()), raw);
        }
        return PayRefundResult.failure(state.getNo(), "MOCK_FAIL", "模拟退款失败", raw);
    }

    private static LocalDateTime toTime(Long millis) {
        return millis == null ? null : LocalDateTimeUtil.of(millis);
    }

    private void save(MockPayState state) {
        redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(String.format(KEY_FORMAT, state.getType(), state.getNo()), JsonUtils.toJsonString(state), TTL);
    }

    private MockPayState load(String type, String no) {
        String json = redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(String.format(KEY_FORMAT, type, no));
        return json == null ? null : JsonUtils.parseObject(json, MockPayState.class);
    }
}
```

`MockPayClientProvider`（`@Component @ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "ccloud.pay", name = "mock-enabled", havingValue = "true")`）：编码 `PayChannelEnum.MOCK.getCode()`，配置类 `MockPayClientConfig.class`，`create` 返回 `new MockPayClient(channelId, (MockPayClientConfig) config, stringRedisTemplate, sender)`。

- Modify: `{pbiz}/service/app/PayAppServiceImpl.java`（`deleteApp` 检查支付单）、`{ptest}/api/RpcApiCoverageTest.java`（移除 `PayOrderApi`）
- Test: `{ptest}/service/order/PayOrderServiceImplTest.java`、`{ptest}/service/order/PayOrderConcurrencyTest.java`、`{ptest}/controller/admin/order/PayOrderControllerTest.java`、`{ptest}/api/order/PayOrderApiImplTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PayAppService`、`PayChannelService`（Task 5）；`PayNoRedisDAO`（Task 4）；`OutboxMessageService`；`PayProperties`。
- Produces: 共享约定中的 `PayOrderService`；`PayOrderSubmitReqVO`（`@NotBlank String channelCode; @Size(max = 32) String payMethod; Map<String, String> channelExtras;`）与 `PayOrderSubmitRespVO`（`Integer status; String displayMode; String displayContent; String no;`），会员端与开放接口复用。

- [ ] **Step 1: DO 与 Mapper**

`PayOrderDO`（`pay_order`）：`Long id; Long appId; Long channelId; String channelCode; String payMethod; String merchantOrderId; String subject; String body; Integer price; Double channelFeeRate; Integer channelFeePrice; Integer status; String userIp; LocalDateTime expireTime; LocalDateTime successTime; Long extensionId; String no; Integer refundPrice; String channelUserId; String channelOrderNo; Long userId; Integer userType;`

`PayOrderExtensionDO`（`@TableName(value = "pay_order_extension", autoResultMap = true)`）：`Long id; String no; Long orderId; Long channelId; String channelCode; String payMethod; String userIp; Integer status; @TableField(typeHandler = JacksonTypeHandler.class) Map<String, String> channelExtras; String channelErrorCode; String channelErrorMsg; String channelNotifyData;`

```java
@Mapper
public interface PayOrderMapper extends BaseMapperX<PayOrderDO> {

    default PayOrderDO selectByAppIdAndMerchantOrderId(Long appId, String merchantOrderId) {
        return selectOne(PayOrderDO::getAppId, appId, PayOrderDO::getMerchantOrderId, merchantOrderId);
    }

    default PageResult<PayOrderDO> selectPage(PayOrderPageReqVO reqVO) {
        return selectPage(reqVO, new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayOrderDO>()
                .eqIfPresent(PayOrderDO::getAppId, reqVO.getAppId())
                .eqIfPresent(PayOrderDO::getChannelCode, reqVO.getChannelCode())
                .eqIfPresent(PayOrderDO::getMerchantOrderId, reqVO.getMerchantOrderId())
                .eqIfPresent(PayOrderDO::getNo, reqVO.getNo())
                .eqIfPresent(PayOrderDO::getStatus, reqVO.getStatus())
                .betweenIfPresent(PayOrderDO::getCreateTime, reqVO.getCreateTime())
                .orderByDesc(PayOrderDO::getId));
    }

    default int updateByIdAndStatus(Long id, Integer whereStatus, PayOrderDO update) {
        return update(update, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<PayOrderDO>().eq(PayOrderDO::getId, id).eq(PayOrderDO::getStatus, whereStatus));
    }

    default List<PayOrderDO> selectListByStatusAndExpireTimeLt(Integer status, LocalDateTime time, int limit) {
        return selectList(new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayOrderDO>().eq(PayOrderDO::getStatus, status)
                .lt(PayOrderDO::getExpireTime, time).orderByAsc(PayOrderDO::getId).last("LIMIT " + limit));
    }

    default Long selectCountByAppId(Long appId) {
        return selectCount(PayOrderDO::getAppId, appId);
    }

    /**
     * 预占退款额度：只有支付成功的订单，且累计退款加本次不超过支付金额时才成功
     */
    @Update("UPDATE pay_order SET refund_price = refund_price + #{price} WHERE id = #{id} AND deleted = 0 "
            + "AND status = 10 AND refund_price + #{price} <= price")
    int reserveRefundPrice(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("price") int price);

    /**
     * 退款失败时释放预占的额度
     */
    @Update("UPDATE pay_order SET refund_price = refund_price - #{price} WHERE id = #{id} AND deleted = 0 AND refund_price >= #{price}")
    int releaseRefundPrice(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("price") int price);
}
```

```java
@Mapper
public interface PayOrderExtensionMapper extends BaseMapperX<PayOrderExtensionDO> {

    default PayOrderExtensionDO selectByNo(String no) {
        return selectOne(PayOrderExtensionDO::getNo, no);
    }

    default List<PayOrderExtensionDO> selectListByOrderId(Long orderId) {
        return selectList(PayOrderExtensionDO::getOrderId, orderId);
    }

    default List<PayOrderExtensionDO> selectListByOrderIdAndStatus(Long orderId, Integer status) {
        return selectList(new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayOrderExtensionDO>().eq(PayOrderExtensionDO::getOrderId, orderId)
                .eq(PayOrderExtensionDO::getStatus, status));
    }

    default int updateByIdAndStatus(Long id, Integer whereStatus, PayOrderExtensionDO update) {
        return update(update, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<PayOrderExtensionDO>().eq(PayOrderExtensionDO::getId, id)
                .eq(PayOrderExtensionDO::getStatus, whereStatus));
    }

    default List<PayOrderExtensionDO> selectListByStatusAndCreateTimeBetween(Integer status, LocalDateTime from,
                                                                            LocalDateTime to, int limit) {
        return selectList(new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayOrderExtensionDO>().eq(PayOrderExtensionDO::getStatus, status)
                .between(PayOrderExtensionDO::getCreateTime, from, to).orderByAsc(PayOrderExtensionDO::getId)
                .last("LIMIT " + limit));
    }

    /**
     * 异常支付：支付尝试成功，但订单记录的成功尝试不是它（重复支付，或订单关闭后才付款成功）
     */
    default PageResult<PayOrderExtensionDO> selectAbnormalPage(PageParam pageParam) {
        return selectPage(pageParam, new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayOrderExtensionDO>()
                .eq(PayOrderExtensionDO::getStatus, PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus())
                .apply("NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pay_order o WHERE o.id = pay_order_extension.order_id "
                        + "AND o.extension_id = pay_order_extension.id AND o.deleted = 0)")
                .orderByDesc(PayOrderExtensionDO::getId));
    }
}
```

```java
@Import({PayOrderServiceImpl.class, PayAppServiceImpl.class, PayChannelServiceImpl.class, PayClientFactory.class,
        FakePayClientProvider.class, PayNoRedisDAO.class, PayProperties.class})
class PayOrderServiceImplTest extends BasePayDbAndRedisUnitTest {

    @Resource
    private PayOrderServiceImpl orderService;
    @Resource
    private PayAppServiceImpl appService;
    @Resource
    private PayOrderMapper orderMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayOrderExtensionMapper extensionMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayAppMapper appMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayChannelMapper channelMapper;
    @Resource
    private FakePayClientProvider fakeProvider;
    @Resource
    private JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
    @MockitoBean
    private OutboxMessageService outboxMessageService;
    @MockitoBean
    private OpenAppApi openAppApi;

    private PayAppDO app;
    private PayChannelDO channel;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        fakeProvider.client().reset();
        app = new PayAppDO().setAppKey("ut-mall").setName("商城").setStatus(0);
        appMapper.insert(app);
        channel = new PayChannelDO().setAppId(app.getId()).setCode("mock").setStatus(0).setFeeRate(0.6).setConfig("{}");
        channelMapper.insert(channel);
    }

    private PayOrderCreateReqDTO orderReq(String merchantOrderId, int price) {
        return new PayOrderCreateReqDTO().setAppKey("ut-mall").setMerchantOrderId(merchantOrderId).setSubject("测试商品")
                .setPrice(price).setExpireTime(LocalDateTime.now().plusHours(1)).setUserIp("127.0.0.1")
                .setUserId(80001L).setUserType(UserTypeEnum.MEMBER.getValue());
    }

    private PayOrderSubmitReqVO submit(String code) {
        return new PayOrderSubmitReqVO().setChannelCode(code);
    }

    /** 提交支付并返回本次支付尝试 */
    private PayOrderExtensionDO submitAndGetExtension(Long orderId) {
        PayOrderSubmitRespVO resp = orderService.submitOrder(orderId, submit("mock"), "127.0.0.1");
        return extensionMapper.selectByNo(resp.getNo());
    }

    private PayOrderResult success(PayOrderExtensionDO ext, int price) {
        return PayOrderResult.success(ext.getNo(), "CH-" + ext.getNo(), price, LocalDateTime.now(), "{\"raw\":1}");
    }

    @Test
    void 创建支付单_重复创建金额相同返回原单_金额不同报错() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M1", 100));
        assertThat(orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M1", 100))).isEqualTo(id);
        assertServiceException(() -> orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M1", 101)), PAY_ORDER_EXISTS_PRICE_MISMATCH);
        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        assertThat(order.getRefundPrice()).isZero();
        assertThat(order.getUserId()).isEqualTo(80001L);
    }

    @Test
    void 提交支付返回渠道展示信息_写入等待中的支付尝试_回调地址指向本渠道() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M2", 100));
        PayOrderSubmitRespVO resp = orderService.submitOrder(id, submit("mock").setPayMethod("wx_lite"), "10.0.0.1");
        assertThat(resp.getDisplayMode()).isEqualTo("URL");
        assertThat(resp.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = extensionMapper.selectByNo(resp.getNo());
        assertThat(ext.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        assertThat(ext.getPayMethod()).isEqualTo("wx_lite");
        PayOrderUnifiedReq req = fakeProvider.client().lastOrderReq;
        assertThat(req.getNotifyUrl()).endsWith("/app-api/pay/notify/order/" + channel.getId());
        assertThat(req.getPrice()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(req.getPayMethod()).isEqualTo("wx_lite");
    }

    @Test
    void 已过期或非待支付的订单不能提交() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M3", 100));
        orderMapper.updateById(new PayOrderDO().setId(id).setExpireTime(LocalDateTime.now().minusMinutes(1)));
        assertServiceException(() -> orderService.submitOrder(id, submit("mock"), "1.1.1.1"), PAY_ORDER_IS_EXPIRED);
        orderMapper.updateById(new PayOrderDO().setId(id).setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus()));
        assertServiceException(() -> orderService.submitOrder(id, submit("mock"), "1.1.1.1"), PAY_ORDER_STATUS_IS_NOT_WAITING);
        assertServiceException(() -> orderService.submitOrder(-1L, submit("mock"), "1.1.1.1"), PAY_ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
    }

    @Test
    void 渠道调用异常时支付尝试保持等待并提示稍后查询() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M4", 100));
        fakeProvider.client().throwOnCall = new IllegalStateException("timeout");
        assertServiceException(() -> orderService.submitOrder(id, submit("mock"), "1.1.1.1"), PAY_ORDER_SUBMIT_CHANNEL_ERROR);
        assertThat(extensionMapper.selectListByOrderId(id)).singleElement()
                .extracting(PayOrderExtensionDO::getStatus).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void 渠道同步返回失败时支付尝试关闭并提示原因_订单仍可换渠道() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M5", 100));
        fakeProvider.client().onUnifiedOrder = req -> PayOrderResult.failure(req.getOutTradeNo(), "E1", "卡被拒绝", "{}");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> orderService.submitOrder(id, submit("mock"), "1.1.1.1"))
                .isInstanceOf(ServiceException.class).hasMessageContaining("卡被拒绝");
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = extensionMapper.selectListByOrderId(id).get(0);
        assertThat(ext.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus());
        assertThat(ext.getChannelErrorMsg()).isEqualTo("卡被拒绝");
        assertThat(orderMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void 支付成功_订单写入渠道与手续费_写一条支付成功消息() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M6", 1000));
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = submitAndGetExtension(id);
        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, success(ext, 1000));

        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus());
        assertThat(order.getExtensionId()).isEqualTo(ext.getId());
        assertThat(order.getNo()).isEqualTo(ext.getNo());
        assertThat(order.getChannelCode()).isEqualTo("mock");
        assertThat(order.getChannelFeePrice()).isEqualTo(6);
        assertThat(extensionMapper.selectById(ext.getId()).getChannelNotifyData()).isEqualTo("{\"raw\":1}");

        ArgumentCaptor<Object> payload = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(Object.class);
        verify(outboxMessageService).saveAndPublish(eq(PayOrderMessage.TOPIC_PAID), payload.capture());
        PayOrderMessage message = (PayOrderMessage) payload.getValue();
        assertThat(message.getAppKey()).isEqualTo("ut-mall");
        assertThat(message.getMerchantOrderId()).isEqualTo("M6");
        assertThat(message.getTenantId()).isEqualTo(TENANT_ID);
        assertThat(message.getPrice()).isEqualTo(1000);
    }

    @Test
    void 重复通知只处理一次() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M7", 100));
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = submitAndGetExtension(id);
        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, success(ext, 100));
        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, success(ext, 100));
        verify(outboxMessageService, times(1)).saveAndPublish(eq(PayOrderMessage.TOPIC_PAID), any());
    }

    @Test
    void 回调金额与订单不一致时拒绝且不改状态() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M8", 100));
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = submitAndGetExtension(id);
        assertServiceException(() -> orderService.notifyOrder(channel, success(ext, 1)), PAY_ORDER_NOTIFY_PRICE_MISMATCH);
        assertThat(extensionMapper.selectById(ext.getId()).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        assertThat(orderMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        verify(outboxMessageService, never()).saveAndPublish(any(), any());
    }

    @Test
    void 订单已关闭后支付成功记为异常支付() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M9", 100));
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = submitAndGetExtension(id);
        orderMapper.updateById(new PayOrderDO().setId(id).setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus()));
        extensionMapper.updateById(new PayOrderExtensionDO().setId(ext.getId()).setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus()));

        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, success(ext, 100));

        assertThat(extensionMapper.selectById(ext.getId()).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus());
        assertThat(orderMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus());
        assertThat(orderService.getAbnormalExtensionPage(new PageParam()).getList())
                .extracting(PayOrderExtensionDO::getId).contains(ext.getId());
        verify(outboxMessageService, never()).saveAndPublish(any(), any());
    }

    @Test
    void 同一订单第二笔支付成功记为异常支付() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M10", 100));
        PayOrderExtensionDO first = submitAndGetExtension(id);
        PayOrderExtensionDO second = submitAndGetExtension(id);
        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, success(first, 100));
        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, success(second, 100));
        assertThat(orderMapper.selectById(id).getExtensionId()).isEqualTo(first.getId());
        assertThat(orderService.getAbnormalExtensionPage(new PageParam()).getList())
                .extracting(PayOrderExtensionDO::getId).containsExactly(second.getId());
    }

    @Test
    void 钱包支付仅限会员本人订单_充值订单不能用钱包() {
        channelMapper.insert(new PayChannelDO().setAppId(app.getId()).setCode("wallet").setStatus(0).setFeeRate(0D).setConfig("{}"));

    @Test
    void 补查_等待中的支付尝试查到成功后更新订单() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M12", 100));
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = submitAndGetExtension(id);
        jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE pay_order_extension SET create_time = ? WHERE id = ?", LocalDateTime.now().minusMinutes(5), ext.getId());
        fakeProvider.client().onGetOrder = no -> PayOrderResult.success(no, "CH", 100, LocalDateTime.now(), "{}");

        assertThat(orderService.syncOrder(100)).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(1);

    @Test
    void 超时关单_渠道查到已支付则改为成功不关闭() {
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M13", 100));
        submitAndGetExtension(id);

    @Test

        Long id = orderService.createOrder(orderReq("M14", 100));
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = submitAndGetExtension(id);

        assertThat(extensionMapper.selectById(ext.getId()).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus());
```

（`syncOrder` 与 `expireOrder` 跨租户扫描，测试库中若有其他用例遗留的数据也会被处理，所以断言用 `isGreaterThanOrEqualTo`，并按本用例的订单核对状态。）

`PayOrderConcurrencyTest`（同样的 `@Import` 与桩，单独一个类是因为方法须在事务外运行）：

```java
    @Test
    @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NOT_SUPPORTED)
    void 并发通知同一笔支付只入账一次() throws Exception {
        // 在 TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, ...) 中建应用 ut-conc、mock 渠道、订单 MC1 并提交，取得支付尝试 ext
        try {
            ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(8);
            List<Future<?>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
                futures.add(pool.submit(() -> TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, () ->
                        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, PayOrderResult.success(ext.getNo(), "CH", 100, LocalDateTime.now(), "{}")))));
            }
            for (Future<?> f : futures) f.get();
            pool.shutdown();
            verify(outboxMessageService, times(1)).saveAndPublish(eq(PayOrderMessage.TOPIC_PAID), any());
            assertThat(TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, () -> orderMapper.selectById(ext.getOrderId())).getExtensionId())
                    .isEqualTo(ext.getId());
        } finally {
            jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_order_extension WHERE order_id = ?", ext.getOrderId());
            jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_order WHERE id = ?", ext.getOrderId());
            jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_channel WHERE app_id = ?", app.getId());
            jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_app WHERE id = ?", app.getId());
        }
    }
```

Run: `mvn -o -q -pl ccloud-module-pay/ccloud-module-pay-biz -am test -Dtest='PayOrder*Test' -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
Expected: FAIL（编译失败）。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现服务**

```java
@Service
@Validated
@Slf4j
public class PayOrderServiceImpl implements PayOrderService {

    @Resource
    private PayOrderMapper orderMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayOrderExtensionMapper extensionMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayAppService appService;
    @Resource
    private PayChannelService channelService;
    @Resource
    private PayNoRedisDAO noRedisDAO;
    @Resource
    private OutboxMessageService outboxMessageService;
    @Resource
    private PayProperties payProperties;
    @Resource
    private TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    @Override
    public Long createOrder(PayOrderCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        return createOrder(appService.validPayApp(reqDTO.getAppKey()), reqDTO);
    }

    @Override
    public Long createOrder(PayAppDO app, PayOrderCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        PayOrderDO existing = orderMapper.selectByAppIdAndMerchantOrderId(app.getId(), reqDTO.getMerchantOrderId());
        if (existing != null) {
            return sameOrder(existing, reqDTO);
        }
        PayOrderDO order = new PayOrderDO().setAppId(app.getId()).setMerchantOrderId(reqDTO.getMerchantOrderId())
                .setSubject(reqDTO.getSubject()).setBody(StrUtil.nullToEmpty(reqDTO.getBody())).setPrice(reqDTO.getPrice())
                .setExpireTime(reqDTO.getExpireTime()).setUserIp(reqDTO.getUserIp())
                .setUserId(reqDTO.getUserId()).setUserType(reqDTO.getUserType())
                .setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus()).setRefundPrice(0).setChannelFeePrice(0);
        try {
            orderMapper.insert(order);
            return order.getId();
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            // 并发重复创建：以先写入的为准
            return sameOrder(orderMapper.selectByAppIdAndMerchantOrderId(app.getId(), reqDTO.getMerchantOrderId()), reqDTO);
        }
    }

    /** 同一商户订单号重复创建：金额一致返回原单，不一致说明业务方用同一单号改了价，拒绝 */
    private static Long sameOrder(PayOrderDO existing, PayOrderCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        if (!existing.getPrice().equals(reqDTO.getPrice())) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_EXISTS_PRICE_MISMATCH);
        }
        return existing.getId();
    }

    @Override
    public PayOrderSubmitRespVO submitOrder(Long orderId, PayOrderSubmitReqVO reqVO, String userIp) {
        PayOrderDO order = validateOrderCanSubmit(orderId);
        PayChannelDO channel = channelService.validPayChannel(order.getAppId(), reqVO.getChannelCode());
        if (PayChannelEnum.WALLET.getCode().equals(channel.getCode())) {
            if (order.getUserId() == null || !UserTypeEnum.MEMBER.getValue().equals(order.getUserType())) {
                throw exception(PAY_ORDER_WALLET_REQUIRE_MEMBER);
            }
            if (isRechargeApp(order.getAppId())) {
                throw exception(PAY_ORDER_RECHARGE_NOT_ALLOW_WALLET);
            }
        }
        // 支付尝试先落库：渠道调用结果未知时，补查任务凭它向渠道查单
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = new PayOrderExtensionDO().setNo(noRedisDAO.generate(PayNoRedisDAO.ORDER_PREFIX))
                .setOrderId(order.getId()).setChannelId(channel.getId()).setChannelCode(channel.getCode())
                .setPayMethod(reqVO.getPayMethod()).setUserIp(userIp).setChannelExtras(reqVO.getChannelExtras())
                .setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        extensionMapper.insert(ext);

        PayOrderResult result;
        try {
            result = channelService.getPayClient(channel).unifiedOrder(new PayOrderUnifiedReq()
                    .setOutTradeNo(ext.getNo()).setSubject(order.getSubject()).setBody(order.getBody())
                    .setPrice(order.getPrice()).setExpireTime(order.getExpireTime())
                    .setNotifyUrl(channelService.buildNotifyUrl("order", channel.getId())).setUserIp(userIp)
                    .setPayMethod(reqVO.getPayMethod()).setUserId(order.getUserId()).setUserType(order.getUserType())
                    .setChannelExtras(reqVO.getChannelExtras()));
        } catch (ServiceException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[submitOrder] 支付尝试 {} 调用渠道异常，结果未知，等待补查", ext.getNo(), e);
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_SUBMIT_CHANNEL_ERROR, "渠道暂时不可用，请稍后查询支付结果");
        }
        if (result.getStatus() != PayClientStatus.WAITING) {
            notifyOrder(channel, result);
        }
        if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.FAILURE) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_SUBMIT_CHANNEL_ERROR, StrUtil.blankToDefault(result.getErrorMsg(), "渠道返回失败"));
        }
        return new PayOrderSubmitRespVO().setStatus(orderMapper.selectById(orderId).getStatus()).setNo(ext.getNo())
                .setDisplayMode(result.getDisplayMode() == null ? null : result.getDisplayMode().name())
                .setDisplayContent(result.getDisplayContent());
    }

    private PayOrderDO validateOrderCanSubmit(Long orderId) {
        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectById(orderId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        if (!PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus().equals(order.getStatus())) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_STATUS_IS_NOT_WAITING);
        }
        if (order.getExpireTime().isBefore(LocalDateTime.now())) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_IS_EXPIRED);
        }
        return order;
    }

    /** 钱包充值订单不能用钱包付。这里直接比对应用标识，不依赖充值服务（充值服务依赖本服务） */
    boolean isRechargeApp(Long appId) {
        PayAppDO app = appService.getApp(appId);
        return app != null && app.getAppKey().equals(payProperties.getWallet().getRechargeAppKey());
    }

    /**
     * 回调、同步结果、补查共用。自带事务（经 TransactionTemplate），本类内部调用也生效
     */
    @Override
    public void notifyOrder(PayChannelDO channel, PayOrderResult result) {
        transactionTemplate.executeWithoutResult(status -> {
            PayOrderExtensionDO ext = extensionMapper.selectByNo(result.getOutTradeNo());
            if (ext == null) {
                throw exception(PAY_ORDER_EXTENSION_NOT_FOUND);
            }
            if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.SUCCESS) {
                notifyOrderSuccess(channel, ext, result);
            } else if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.FAILURE) {
                notifyOrderClosed(ext, result);
            }
        });
    }

    private void notifyOrderSuccess(PayChannelDO channel, PayOrderExtensionDO ext, PayOrderResult result) {
        if (PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus().equals(ext.getStatus())) {
            return;
        }
        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectById(ext.getOrderId());
        if (!Objects.equals(result.getPrice(), order.getPrice())) {
            log.error("[notifyOrderSuccess] 支付尝试 {} 渠道金额 {} 与订单金额 {} 不一致，拒绝", ext.getNo(), result.getPrice(), order.getPrice());
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_NOTIFY_PRICE_MISMATCH);
        }
        // 关闭的支付尝试也接受成功：关单后用户才付款，钱已到账，必须记下来
        int updated = extensionMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(ext.getId(), ext.getStatus(), new PayOrderExtensionDO()
                .setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus())
                .setChannelNotifyData(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getRawData(), 4000)));
        if (updated == 0) {
            return;
        }
        PayOrderDO update = new PayOrderDO().setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus())
                .setChannelId(channel.getId()).setChannelCode(channel.getCode()).setPayMethod(ext.getPayMethod())
                .setSuccessTime(ObjectUtil.defaultIfNull(result.getSuccessTime(), LocalDateTime.now()))
                .setExtensionId(ext.getId()).setNo(ext.getNo())
                .setChannelOrderNo(result.getChannelOrderNo()).setChannelUserId(result.getChannelUserId())
                .setChannelFeeRate(channel.getFeeRate())
                .setChannelFeePrice(MoneyUtils.calculateRatePrice(order.getPrice(), channel.getFeeRate()));
        if (orderMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(order.getId(), PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), update) == 0) {
            log.error("[notifyOrderSuccess] 订单 {} 已不是待支付，支付尝试 {} 记为异常支付，需人工处理", order.getId(), ext.getNo());
            return;
        }
        PayAppDO app = appService.getApp(order.getAppId());
        outboxMessageService.saveAndPublish(PayOrderMessage.TOPIC_PAID, new PayOrderMessage()
                .setTenantId(TenantContextHolder.getTenantId()).setAppId(app.getId()).setAppKey(app.getAppKey())
                .setOrderId(order.getId()).setOrderNo(ext.getNo()).setMerchantOrderId(order.getMerchantOrderId())
                .setPrice(order.getPrice()).setChannelCode(channel.getCode()).setPayMethod(ext.getPayMethod())
                .setSuccessTime(update.getSuccessTime()).setUserId(order.getUserId()).setUserType(order.getUserType()));
    }

    private void notifyOrderClosed(PayOrderExtensionDO ext, PayOrderResult result) {
        extensionMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(ext.getId(), PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), new PayOrderExtensionDO()
                .setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus()).setChannelErrorCode(result.getErrorCode())
                .setChannelErrorMsg(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getErrorMsg(), 250))
                .setChannelNotifyData(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getRawData(), 4000)));
    }

    @Override
    public int syncOrder(int limit) {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        List<PayOrderExtensionDO> extensions = TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> extensionMapper.selectListByStatusAndCreateTimeBetween(
                PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), now.minus(payProperties.getJob().getSyncWindow()),
                now.minus(payProperties.getJob().getSyncDelay()), limit));
        int count = 0;
        for (PayOrderExtensionDO ext : extensions) {
            try {
                TenantUtils.execute(ext.getTenantId(), () -> syncExtension(ext));
                count++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[syncOrder] 支付尝试 {} 补查失败", ext.getNo(), e);
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    private void syncExtension(PayOrderExtensionDO ext) {
        PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannel(ext.getChannelId());
        if (channel == null) {
            return;
        }
        PayOrderResult result = channelService.getPayClient(channel).getOrder(ext.getNo());
        if (result.getStatus() != PayClientStatus.WAITING) {
            notifyOrder(channel, result);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public int expireOrder(int limit) {
        List<PayOrderDO> orders = TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> orderMapper.selectListByStatusAndExpireTimeLt(
                PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), LocalDateTime.now(), limit));
        int closed = 0;
        for (PayOrderDO order : orders) {
            try {
                if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(TenantUtils.execute(order.getTenantId(), () -> expireOne(order)))) {
                    closed++;
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[expireOrder] 订单 {} 关闭失败", order.getId(), e);
            }
        }
        return closed;
    }

    /**
     * 先向渠道确认每个等待中的支付尝试都未支付，才关闭订单；查询出错则本次跳过，下次再试
     */
    private boolean expireOne(PayOrderDO order) {
        for (PayOrderExtensionDO ext : extensionMapper.selectListByOrderIdAndStatus(order.getId(), PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus())) {
            PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannel(ext.getChannelId());
            if (channel != null) {
                PayOrderResult result = channelService.getPayClient(channel).getOrder(ext.getNo());
                if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.SUCCESS) {
                    notifyOrder(channel, result);
                    return false;
                }
            }
            extensionMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(ext.getId(), PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), new PayOrderExtensionDO()
                    .setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus()).setChannelErrorMsg("订单超时关闭"));
        }
        return orderMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(order.getId(), PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(),
                new PayOrderDO().setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.CLOSED.getStatus())) > 0;
    }

    // getOrder、getOrder(appId, merchantOrderId)、getOrderExtension、getOrderExtensionListByOrderId、getOrderPage、
    // getAbnormalExtensionPage 直接委托 Mapper；getOrderExtensionByNoIgnoreTenant 用 TenantUtils.executeIgnore
}
```

（`TenantUtils.execute(Long, ...)` 带返回值的重载以框架为准；若只有 `Runnable` 版本，用局部的 `AtomicBoolean` 收集结果。）

```java
class PayNotifyControllerTest extends BasePayWebIntegrationTest {

    private static final String SECRET = "0123456789abcdef-webtest";

    @Resource
    private PayAppMapper appMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayChannelMapper channelMapper;
    @Resource
    private PayOrderService orderService;

    private PayChannelDO channel;
    private String no;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        TenantUtils.execute(TENANT, () -> {
            PayAppDO app = new PayAppDO().setAppKey("webtest-notify").setName("回调测试").setStatus(0);
            appMapper.insert(app);
            channel = new PayChannelDO().setAppId(app.getId()).setCode("mock").setStatus(0).setFeeRate(0D)
                    .setConfig("{\"notifySecret\":\"" + SECRET + "\",\"behavior\":\"DROP_NOTIFY\",\"delaySeconds\":1}");
            channelMapper.insert(channel);
            Long orderId = orderService.createOrder(new PayOrderCreateReqDTO().setAppKey("webtest-notify")
                    .setMerchantOrderId("WT-N1").setSubject("回调").setPrice(100)
                    .setExpireTime(LocalDateTime.now().plusHours(1)).setUserIp("127.0.0.1"));
            no = orderService.submitOrder(orderId, new PayOrderSubmitReqVO().setChannelCode("mock"), "127.0.0.1").getNo();
        });
    }

    @AfterEach
    void cleanup() {
        jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE e FROM pay_order_extension e JOIN pay_order o ON e.order_id = o.id "
                + "JOIN pay_app a ON o.app_id = a.id WHERE a.app_key LIKE 'webtest-%'");
        jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE o FROM pay_order o JOIN pay_app a ON o.app_id = a.id WHERE a.app_key LIKE 'webtest-%'");
        jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE c FROM pay_channel c JOIN pay_app a ON c.app_id = a.id WHERE a.app_key LIKE 'webtest-%'");
        jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM pay_app WHERE app_key LIKE 'webtest-%'");
        jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM infra_message_outbox WHERE payload LIKE '%WT-%'");
    }

    private MockHttpServletRequestBuilder signedNotify(String body, String secret) {
        long ts = System.currentTimeMillis();
        return post("/app-api/pay/notify/order/" + channel.getId()).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content(body)
                .header("X-Mock-Timestamp", ts).header("X-Mock-Sign", MockPayNotifySigner.sign(secret, ts, body));
    }

    private String body(int price) {
        return "{\"type\":\"order\",\"no\":\"" + no + "\",\"status\":\"SUCCESS\",\"channelNo\":\"MOCK-" + no
                + "\",\"price\":" + price + ",\"successTime\":" + System.currentTimeMillis() + "}";
    }

    @Test
    void 验签通过后订单支付成功_不带登录与租户头_应答success() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(signedNotify(body(100), SECRET))
                .andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(content().string("success"));
        Integer status = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT status FROM pay_order WHERE merchant_order_id = 'WT-N1'", Integer.class);
        assertThat(status).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void 验签失败返回400且订单不变() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(signedNotify(body(100), "wrong-secret-000000")).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        Integer status = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT status FROM pay_order WHERE merchant_order_id = 'WT-N1'", Integer.class);
        assertThat(status).isEqualTo(PayOrderStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void 金额不一致返回400() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(signedNotify(body(1), SECRET)).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }

    @Test
    void 渠道不存在或类型未知返回404() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/app-api/pay/notify/order/999999999").content("{}")).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mockMvc.perform(post("/app-api/pay/notify/unknown/" + channel.getId()).content("{}")).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 实现回调入口**

```java
@Tag(name = "渠道回调")
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/app-api/pay/notify")
@Slf4j
public class PayNotifyController {

    @Resource
    private PayChannelService channelService;
    @Resource
    private PayOrderService orderService;

    /**
     * 免登录、免租户头：按渠道确定租户，按渠道实现验签。成功应答渠道要求的报文；
     * 验签失败或业务校验不通过返回 400（渠道会重试，无害）；其他异常返回 500，等渠道重试
     */
    @PostMapping("/{type}/{channelId}")
    @Operation(summary = "接收渠道回调")
    public ResponseEntity<String> notify(@PathVariable("type") String type, @PathVariable("channelId") Long channelId,
                                         @RequestParam Map<String, String> params,
                                         @RequestBody(required = false) String body, HttpServletRequest request) {
        PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannelIgnoreTenant(channelId);
        if (channel == null || !List.of("order", "refund", "transfer").contains(type)) {
            return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND).body("not found");
        }
        Map<String, String> headers = new HashMap<>();
        Collections.list(request.getHeaderNames()).forEach(name -> headers.put(name.toLowerCase(), request.getHeader(name)));
        PayNotifyRequest notifyRequest = new PayNotifyRequest(params, headers, StrUtil.nullToEmpty(body));
        try {
            String ack = TenantUtils.execute(channel.getTenantId(), () -> handle(type, channel, notifyRequest));
            return ResponseEntity.ok().contentType(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN).body(ack);
        } catch (PayNotifyVerifyException e) {
            log.warn("[notify] 渠道 {} 的 {} 回调验签失败：{}", channelId, type, e.getMessage());
            return ResponseEntity.badRequest().body("invalid notify");
        } catch (ServiceException e) {
            log.error("[notify] 渠道 {} 的 {} 回调处理被拒绝：{}", channelId, type, e.getMessage());
            return ResponseEntity.badRequest().body(e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private String handle(String type, PayChannelDO channel, PayNotifyRequest request) {
        PayClient client = channelService.getPayClient(channel);
        switch (type) {
            case "order" -> orderService.notifyOrder(channel, client.parseOrderNotify(request));
            default -> throw new PayNotifyVerifyException("暂不支持的回调类型：" + type);
        }
        return client.notifyAckBody();
    }
}
```

（`TenantUtils.execute` 带返回值的重载以框架为准。Task 10、11 在 `switch` 中加 `refund`、`transfer` 分支。）

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 Mock 确认页**

```java
@Tag(name = "Mock 支付确认页")
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/app-api/pay/mock/confirm")
@ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "ccloud.pay", name = "mock-enabled", havingValue = "true")
public class PayMockConfirmController {

    @Resource
    private PayOrderService orderService;
    @Resource
    private PayChannelService channelService;

    @GetMapping(produces = MediaType.TEXT_HTML_VALUE)
    @Operation(summary = "模拟支付确认页", description = "仅开启模拟支付的环境可用。展示商品与金额，点击按钮后以 POST 确认")
    public ResponseEntity<String> page(@RequestParam("no") String no) {
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = orderService.getOrderExtensionByNoIgnoreTenant(no);
        if (ext == null) {
            return notFound();
        }
        PayOrderDO order = TenantUtils.execute(ext.getTenantId(), () -> orderService.getOrder(ext.getOrderId()));
        String html = "<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><title>模拟支付</title></head><body>"
                + "<h3>模拟支付（仅用于本地调试）</h3>"
                + "<p>商品：" + HtmlUtil.escape(order.getSubject()) + "</p>"
                + "<p>金额：" + MoneyUtils.fenToYuanStr(order.getPrice()) + " 元</p>"
                + "<form method=\"post\" action=\"/app-api/pay/mock/confirm?no=" + URLUtil.encodeQuery(no) + "\">"
                + "<button type=\"submit\">确认支付</button></form></body></html>";
        return ResponseEntity.ok().contentType(MediaType.TEXT_HTML).body(html);
    }

    @PostMapping(produces = MediaType.TEXT_HTML_VALUE)
    @Operation(summary = "确认模拟支付", description = "按 Mock 渠道配置的行为落定结果并回调")
    public ResponseEntity<String> confirm(@RequestParam("no") String no) {
        PayOrderExtensionDO ext = orderService.getOrderExtensionByNoIgnoreTenant(no);
        if (ext == null) {
            return notFound();
        }
        boolean confirmed = TenantUtils.execute(ext.getTenantId(), () -> {
            PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannel(ext.getChannelId());
            PayClient client = channel == null ? null : channelService.getPayClient(channel);
            return client instanceof MockPayClient mock && mock.confirm(no);
        });
        return confirmed ? ResponseEntity.ok().contentType(MediaType.TEXT_HTML)
                .body("<!doctype html><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><p>已确认支付，可以关闭本页。</p>") : notFound();
    }

    private static ResponseEntity<String> notFound() {
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND).contentType(MediaType.TEXT_HTML)
                .body("<!doctype html><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><p>支付不存在或已过期</p>");
    }
}
```

（`HtmlUtil`、`URLUtil` 为 Hutool；`MoneyUtils.fenToYuanStr` 为框架已有。）

```java
    private PayRefundCreateReqDTO refundReq(String merchantRefundId, int price) {
        return new PayRefundCreateReqDTO().setAppKey("ut-refund").setMerchantOrderId("M-PAID")
                .setMerchantRefundId(merchantRefundId).setPrice(price).setReason("不想要了").setUserIp("127.0.0.1");

    @Test
    void 发起退款_预占额度_调用渠道且回调地址指向本渠道() {

        PayRefundDO refund = refundMapper.selectById(id);
        assertThat(refund.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        assertThat(orderMapper.selectById(paidOrderId).getRefundPrice()).isEqualTo(300);
        PayRefundUnifiedReq req = fakeProvider.client().lastRefundReq;
        assertThat(req.getOutRefundNo()).isEqualTo(refund.getNo());
        assertThat(req.getOutTradeNo()).isEqualTo(orderMapper.selectById(paidOrderId).getNo());
        assertThat(req.getNotifyUrl()).endsWith("/app-api/pay/notify/refund/" + channel.getId());
    }

    @Test
```

`PayRefundConcurrencyTest`（同样的类级 `NOT_SUPPORTED`、准备与清理）：

```java
    @Test
    void 并发退款合计不超过支付金额() throws Exception {
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(6);
        List<Future<?>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            String merchantRefundId = "RC" + i;
            futures.add(pool.submit(() -> TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, () -> {
                try {
                    refundService.createRefund(refundReq(merchantRefundId, 300));
                } catch (ServiceException ignored) {
                    // 额度不足或已有退款在处理中
                }
            })));
        }
        for (Future<?> f : futures) f.get();
        pool.shutdown();
        PayOrderDO order = TenantUtils.execute(TENANT_ID, () -> orderMapper.selectById(paidOrderId));
        assertThat(order.getRefundPrice()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1000);
        Integer reserved = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(refund_price), 0) FROM pay_refund WHERE order_id = ?",
                Integer.class, paidOrderId);
        assertThat(reserved).isEqualTo(order.getRefundPrice());
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现服务**

```java
@Service
@Validated
@Slf4j
public class PayRefundServiceImpl implements PayRefundService {

    // 注入：PayRefundMapper refundMapper、PayOrderMapper orderMapper、PayAppService appService、PayChannelService channelService、
    // PayNoRedisDAO noRedisDAO、OutboxMessageService outboxMessageService、PayProperties payProperties、TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate

    private record CreateResult(Long refundId, boolean created) {
    }

    @Override
    public Long createRefund(PayRefundCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        return createRefund(appService.validPayApp(reqDTO.getAppKey()), reqDTO);
    }

    @Override
    public Long createRefund(PayAppDO app, PayRefundCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        CreateResult result = transactionTemplate.execute(status -> doCreateRefund(app, reqDTO));
        if (result.created()) {
            runAfterCommit(() -> invokeRefund(result.refundId()));
        }
        return result.refundId();
    }

    private CreateResult doCreateRefund(PayAppDO app, PayRefundCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectByAppIdAndMerchantOrderId(app.getId(), reqDTO.getMerchantOrderId());
        if (order == null) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        PayRefundDO existing = refundMapper.selectByAppIdAndMerchantRefundId(app.getId(), reqDTO.getMerchantRefundId());
        if (existing != null) {
            if (!existing.getOrderId().equals(order.getId()) || !existing.getRefundPrice().equals(reqDTO.getPrice())) {
                throw exception(PAY_REFUND_EXISTS_MISMATCH);
            }
            return new CreateResult(existing.getId(), false);
        }
        if (!PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus().equals(order.getStatus())) {
            throw exception(PayOrderStatusEnum.REFUND.getStatus().equals(order.getStatus())
                    ? PAY_REFUND_PRICE_EXCEED : PAY_REFUND_ORDER_STATUS_ERROR);
        }
        // 软约束，简化对账；金额不超的硬保证在下面的条件更新
        if (refundMapper.selectCountByOrderIdAndStatus(order.getId(), PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus()) > 0) {
            throw exception(PAY_REFUND_HAS_REFUNDING);
        }
        if (orderMapper.reserveRefundPrice(order.getId(), reqDTO.getPrice()) == 0) {
            throw exception(PAY_REFUND_PRICE_EXCEED);
        }
        PayRefundDO refund = new PayRefundDO().setNo(noRedisDAO.generate(PayNoRedisDAO.REFUND_PREFIX)).setAppId(app.getId())
                .setChannelId(order.getChannelId()).setChannelCode(order.getChannelCode()).setOrderId(order.getId())
                .setOrderNo(order.getNo()).setMerchantOrderId(order.getMerchantOrderId())
                .setMerchantRefundId(reqDTO.getMerchantRefundId()).setStatus(PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus())
                .setPayPrice(order.getPrice()).setRefundPrice(reqDTO.getPrice()).setReason(reqDTO.getReason())
                .setUserIp(reqDTO.getUserIp()).setChannelOrderNo(StrUtil.nullToEmpty(order.getChannelOrderNo()));
        refundMapper.insert(refund);
        return new CreateResult(refund.getId(), true);
    }

    /**
     * 有外层事务时推迟到其提交之后（否则渠道可能退了款而本地记录随外层回滚消失）；没有则立即执行
     */
    private static void runAfterCommit(Runnable task) {
        if (!TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
            task.run();
            return;
        }
        TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
            @Override
            public void afterCommit() {
                task.run();
            }
        });
    }

    private void invokeRefund(Long refundId) {
        PayRefundDO refund = refundMapper.selectById(refundId);
        if (refund == null || !PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus().equals(refund.getStatus())) {
            return;
        }
        PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannel(refund.getChannelId());
        if (channel == null) {
            log.error("[invokeRefund] 退款 {} 的渠道 {} 已删除，无法退款", refund.getNo(), refund.getChannelId());
            return;
        }
        PayRefundResult result;
        try {
            result = channelService.getPayClient(channel).unifiedRefund(new PayRefundUnifiedReq()
                    .setOutTradeNo(refund.getOrderNo()).setOutRefundNo(refund.getNo()).setReason(refund.getReason())
                    .setPayPrice(refund.getPayPrice()).setRefundPrice(refund.getRefundPrice())
                    .setNotifyUrl(channelService.buildNotifyUrl("refund", channel.getId())));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[invokeRefund] 退款 {} 调用渠道异常，等待补查", refund.getNo(), e);
            return;
        }
        if (result.getStatus() != PayClientStatus.WAITING) {
            notifyRefund(channel, result);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void notifyRefund(PayChannelDO channel, PayRefundResult result) {
        transactionTemplate.executeWithoutResult(status -> {
            PayRefundDO refund = refundMapper.selectByNo(result.getOutRefundNo());
            if (refund == null) {
                throw exception(PAY_REFUND_NOT_FOUND);
            }
            if (!PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus().equals(refund.getStatus())) {
                return;
            }
            if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.SUCCESS) {
                notifyRefundSuccess(refund, result);
            } else if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.FAILURE) {
                notifyRefundFailure(refund, result);
            }
        });
    }

    private void notifyRefundSuccess(PayRefundDO refund, PayRefundResult result) {
        if (result.getRefundPrice() != null && !result.getRefundPrice().equals(refund.getRefundPrice())) {
            log.error("[notifyRefundSuccess] 退款 {} 渠道金额 {} 与退款单 {} 不一致，拒绝", refund.getNo(), result.getRefundPrice(), refund.getRefundPrice());
            throw exception(PAY_REFUND_NOTIFY_PRICE_MISMATCH);
        }
        LocalDateTime successTime = ObjectUtil.defaultIfNull(result.getSuccessTime(), LocalDateTime.now());
        if (refundMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(refund.getId(), PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), new PayRefundDO()
                .setStatus(PayRefundStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus()).setChannelRefundNo(result.getChannelRefundNo())
                .setSuccessTime(successTime).setChannelNotifyData(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getRawData(), 4000))) == 0) {
            return;
        }
        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectById(refund.getOrderId());
        if (order.getRefundPrice() >= order.getPrice()) {
            orderMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(order.getId(), PayOrderStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus(),
                    new PayOrderDO().setStatus(PayOrderStatusEnum.REFUND.getStatus()));
        }
        publish(PayRefundMessage.TOPIC_SUCCESS, refund, PayRefundStatusEnum.SUCCESS, successTime, null);
    }

    private void notifyRefundFailure(PayRefundDO refund, PayRefundResult result) {
        if (refundMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(refund.getId(), PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), new PayRefundDO()
                .setStatus(PayRefundStatusEnum.FAILURE.getStatus()).setChannelErrorCode(result.getErrorCode())
                .setChannelErrorMsg(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getErrorMsg(), 250))
                .setChannelNotifyData(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getRawData(), 4000))) == 0) {
            return;
        }
        orderMapper.releaseRefundPrice(refund.getOrderId(), refund.getRefundPrice());
        publish(PayRefundMessage.TOPIC_FAILURE, refund, PayRefundStatusEnum.FAILURE, null, result.getErrorMsg());
    }

    private void publish(String topic, PayRefundDO refund, PayRefundStatusEnum status, LocalDateTime successTime, String errorMsg) {
        PayAppDO app = appService.getApp(refund.getAppId());
        outboxMessageService.saveAndPublish(topic, new PayRefundMessage().setTenantId(TenantContextHolder.getTenantId())
                .setAppId(app.getId()).setAppKey(app.getAppKey()).setRefundId(refund.getId()).setRefundNo(refund.getNo())
                .setOrderId(refund.getOrderId()).setMerchantOrderId(refund.getMerchantOrderId())
                .setMerchantRefundId(refund.getMerchantRefundId()).setRefundPrice(refund.getRefundPrice())
                .setStatus(status.getStatus()).setSuccessTime(successTime).setChannelErrorMsg(errorMsg));
    }

    /**
     * 补查：先查渠道；仍为等待时按同一退款单号重发（渠道按退款单号幂等），覆盖"发起前进程中断"的情况
     */
    @Override
    public int syncRefund(int limit) {
        List<PayRefundDO> refunds = TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> refundMapper.selectListByStatusAndCreateTimeLt(
                PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(), LocalDateTime.now().minus(payProperties.getJob().getSyncDelay()), limit));
        int count = 0;
        for (PayRefundDO refund : refunds) {
            try {
                TenantUtils.execute(refund.getTenantId(), () -> {
                    PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannel(refund.getChannelId());
                    if (channel == null) {
                        return;
                    }
                    PayRefundResult result = channelService.getPayClient(channel).getRefund(refund.getOrderNo(), refund.getNo());
                    if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.WAITING) {
                        invokeRefund(refund.getId());
                    } else {
                        notifyRefund(channel, result);
                    }
                });
                count++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[syncRefund] 退款 {} 补查失败", refund.getNo(), e);
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    // getRefund、getRefund(appId, merchantRefundId)、getRefundPage 直接委托 Mapper
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: DO 与 Mapper**

`PayTransferDO`（`@TableName(value = "pay_transfer", autoResultMap = true)`）：`Long id; String no; Long appId; Long channelId; String channelCode; String merchantTransferId; String subject; Integer price; String userAccount; String userName; Integer status; LocalDateTime successTime; String userIp; @TableField(typeHandler = JacksonTypeHandler.class) Map<String, String> channelExtras; String channelTransferNo; String channelErrorCode; String channelErrorMsg; String channelNotifyData;`

`PayTransferMapper`：`selectByAppIdAndMerchantTransferId`、`selectByNo`、`selectPage(PayTransferPageReqVO)`、`selectListByStatusAndCreateTimeLt(Collection<Integer> statuses, LocalDateTime time, int limit)`，以及：

```java
    default int updateByIdAndStatus(Long id, Collection<Integer> whereStatuses, PayTransferDO update) {
        return update(update, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<PayTransferDO>().eq(PayTransferDO::getId, id)
                .in(PayTransferDO::getStatus, whereStatuses));
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 写服务测试（RED）**

`PayTransferServiceImplTest`（类上 `@Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NOT_SUPPORTED)`，`@AfterEach` 按 `app_key = 'ut-transfer'` 清理；`@Import` 同 Task 8 加 `PayTransferServiceImpl.class`；`@BeforeEach` 在租户 1 下建应用 `ut-transfer` 与 mock 渠道，另插入一条启用的 `wallet` 渠道行，并 `fakeProvider.client().reset()`）：

```java
    private PayTransferCreateReqDTO req(String merchantTransferId, String code, String account) {
        return new PayTransferCreateReqDTO().setAppKey("ut-transfer").setChannelCode(code)
                .setMerchantTransferId(merchantTransferId).setSubject("佣金").setPrice(200)
                .setUserAccount(account).setUserIp("127.0.0.1");
    }

    @Test
    void 发起后渠道受理则为进行中_回调成功后为成功并写消息() {
        Long id = transferService.createTransfer(req("TF1", "mock", "acc-1"));
        PayTransferDO transfer = transferMapper.selectById(id);
        assertThat(transfer.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayTransferStatusEnum.PROCESSING.getStatus());
        assertThat(fakeProvider.client().lastTransferReq.getNotifyUrl()).endsWith("/app-api/pay/notify/transfer/" + channel.getId());

        transferService.notifyTransfer(channel, PayTransferResult.success(transfer.getNo(), "CT", 200, LocalDateTime.now(), "{}"));
        assertThat(transferMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayTransferStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus());
        verify(outboxMessageService).saveAndPublish(eq(PayTransferMessage.TOPIC_SUCCESS), any());
    }

    @Test
    void 重复发起返回原单_金额或账号不同报错() {
        Long id = transferService.createTransfer(req("TF2", "mock", "acc-1"));
        assertThat(transferService.createTransfer(req("TF2", "mock", "acc-1"))).isEqualTo(id);
        assertServiceException(() -> transferService.createTransfer(req("TF2", "mock", "acc-2")), PAY_TRANSFER_EXISTS_MISMATCH);
        assertThat(fakeProvider.client().unifiedTransferCalls).hasValue(1);
    }

    @Test
    void 钱包转账的收款账号须为会员编号() {
        assertServiceException(() -> transferService.createTransfer(req("TF3", "wallet", "abc")), PAY_TRANSFER_WALLET_ACCOUNT_INVALID);
    }

    @Test
    void 回调失败后为失败并写消息_终态后再回调不改变() {
        Long id = transferService.createTransfer(req("TF4", "mock", "acc-1"));
        String no = transferMapper.selectById(id).getNo();
        transferService.notifyTransfer(channel, PayTransferResult.failure(no, "E", "账户异常", "{}"));
        transferService.notifyTransfer(channel, PayTransferResult.success(no, "CT", 200, LocalDateTime.now(), "{}"));
        assertThat(transferMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayTransferStatusEnum.FAILURE.getStatus());
        verify(outboxMessageService).saveAndPublish(eq(PayTransferMessage.TOPIC_FAILURE), any());
        verify(outboxMessageService, never()).saveAndPublish(eq(PayTransferMessage.TOPIC_SUCCESS), any());
    }

    @Test
    void 补查_等待中重发_进行中查询() {
        fakeProvider.client().throwOnCall = new IllegalStateException("timeout");
        Long id = transferService.createTransfer(req("TF5", "mock", "acc-1"));
        assertThat(transferMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayTransferStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        fakeProvider.client().throwOnCall = null;
        jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE pay_transfer SET create_time = ? WHERE id = ?", LocalDateTime.now().minusMinutes(5), id);

        transferService.syncTransfer(100);
        assertThat(transferMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayTransferStatusEnum.PROCESSING.getStatus());

        fakeProvider.client().onGetTransfer = no -> PayTransferResult.success(no, "CT", 200, LocalDateTime.now(), "{}");
        transferService.syncTransfer(100);
        assertThat(transferMapper.selectById(id).getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayTransferStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus());
    }
```

Run: `mvn -o -q -pl ccloud-module-pay/ccloud-module-pay-biz -am test -Dtest='PayTransfer*Test' -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
Expected: FAIL（编译失败）。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现服务**

```java
@Service
@Validated
@Slf4j
public class PayTransferServiceImpl implements PayTransferService {

    private static final List<Integer> UNFINISHED = List.of(PayTransferStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus(),
            PayTransferStatusEnum.PROCESSING.getStatus());

    // 注入：PayTransferMapper transferMapper、PayAppService appService、PayChannelService channelService、PayNoRedisDAO noRedisDAO、
    // OutboxMessageService outboxMessageService、PayProperties payProperties、TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate

    @Override
    public Long createTransfer(PayTransferCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        PayAppDO app = appService.validPayApp(reqDTO.getAppKey());
        PayTransferDO existing = transferMapper.selectByAppIdAndMerchantTransferId(app.getId(), reqDTO.getMerchantTransferId());
        if (existing != null) {
            return sameTransfer(existing, reqDTO);
        }
        PayChannelDO channel = channelService.validPayChannel(app.getId(), reqDTO.getChannelCode());
        if (PayChannelEnum.WALLET.getCode().equals(channel.getCode()) && !NumberUtil.isLong(reqDTO.getUserAccount())) {
            throw exception(PAY_TRANSFER_WALLET_ACCOUNT_INVALID);
        }
        PayTransferDO transfer = new PayTransferDO().setNo(noRedisDAO.generate(PayNoRedisDAO.TRANSFER_PREFIX))
                .setAppId(app.getId()).setChannelId(channel.getId()).setChannelCode(channel.getCode())
                .setMerchantTransferId(reqDTO.getMerchantTransferId()).setSubject(reqDTO.getSubject())
                .setPrice(reqDTO.getPrice()).setUserAccount(reqDTO.getUserAccount()).setUserName(reqDTO.getUserName())
                .setUserIp(reqDTO.getUserIp()).setStatus(PayTransferStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus());
        try {
            transferMapper.insert(transfer);
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            return sameTransfer(transferMapper.selectByAppIdAndMerchantTransferId(app.getId(), reqDTO.getMerchantTransferId()), reqDTO);
        }
        invokeTransfer(transfer.getId());
        return transfer.getId();
    }

    private static Long sameTransfer(PayTransferDO existing, PayTransferCreateReqDTO reqDTO) {
        if (!existing.getPrice().equals(reqDTO.getPrice()) || !existing.getUserAccount().equals(reqDTO.getUserAccount())) {
            throw exception(PAY_TRANSFER_EXISTS_MISMATCH);
        }
        return existing.getId();
    }

    /**
     * 只对等待中的转账调用渠道（渠道按转账单号幂等，重发安全）；渠道受理但未完成时置为进行中
     */
    private void invokeTransfer(Long transferId) {
        PayTransferDO transfer = transferMapper.selectById(transferId);
        if (transfer == null || !PayTransferStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus().equals(transfer.getStatus())) {
            return;
        }
        PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannel(transfer.getChannelId());
        if (channel == null) {
            log.error("[invokeTransfer] 转账 {} 的渠道 {} 已删除", transfer.getNo(), transfer.getChannelId());
            return;
        }
        PayTransferResult result;
        try {
            result = channelService.getPayClient(channel).unifiedTransfer(new PayTransferUnifiedReq()
                    .setOutTransferNo(transfer.getNo()).setSubject(transfer.getSubject()).setPrice(transfer.getPrice())
                    .setUserAccount(transfer.getUserAccount()).setUserName(transfer.getUserName())
                    .setNotifyUrl(channelService.buildNotifyUrl("transfer", channel.getId())));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[invokeTransfer] 转账 {} 调用渠道异常，等待补查", transfer.getNo(), e);
            return;
        }
        if (result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.WAITING) {
            transferMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(transfer.getId(), List.of(PayTransferStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus()),
                    new PayTransferDO().setStatus(PayTransferStatusEnum.PROCESSING.getStatus())
                            .setChannelTransferNo(result.getChannelTransferNo()));
        } else {
            notifyTransfer(channel, result);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void notifyTransfer(PayChannelDO channel, PayTransferResult result) {
        transactionTemplate.executeWithoutResult(status -> {
            PayTransferDO transfer = transferMapper.selectByNo(result.getOutTransferNo());
            if (transfer == null) {
                throw exception(PAY_TRANSFER_NOT_FOUND);
            }
            if (!UNFINISHED.contains(transfer.getStatus()) || result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.WAITING) {
                return;
            }
            boolean success = result.getStatus() == PayClientStatus.SUCCESS;
            LocalDateTime successTime = success ? ObjectUtil.defaultIfNull(result.getSuccessTime(), LocalDateTime.now()) : null;
            PayTransferDO update = new PayTransferDO()
                    .setStatus((success ? PayTransferStatusEnum.SUCCESS : PayTransferStatusEnum.FAILURE).getStatus())
                    .setSuccessTime(successTime).setChannelTransferNo(result.getChannelTransferNo())
                    .setChannelErrorCode(result.getErrorCode()).setChannelErrorMsg(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getErrorMsg(), 500))
                    .setChannelNotifyData(StrUtil.maxLength(result.getRawData(), 4000));
            if (transferMapper.updateByIdAndStatus(transfer.getId(), UNFINISHED, update) == 0) {
                return;
            }
            PayAppDO app = appService.getApp(transfer.getAppId());
            outboxMessageService.saveAndPublish(success ? PayTransferMessage.TOPIC_SUCCESS : PayTransferMessage.TOPIC_FAILURE,
                    new PayTransferMessage().setTenantId(TenantContextHolder.getTenantId()).setAppId(app.getId())
                            .setAppKey(app.getAppKey()).setTransferId(transfer.getId()).setTransferNo(transfer.getNo())
                            .setMerchantTransferId(transfer.getMerchantTransferId()).setPrice(transfer.getPrice())
                            .setStatus(update.getStatus()).setSuccessTime(successTime).setChannelErrorMsg(result.getErrorMsg()));
        });
    }

    @Override
    public int syncTransfer(int limit) {
        List<PayTransferDO> transfers = TenantUtils.executeIgnore(() -> transferMapper.selectListByStatusAndCreateTimeLt(
                UNFINISHED, LocalDateTime.now().minus(payProperties.getJob().getSyncDelay()), limit));
        int count = 0;
        for (PayTransferDO transfer : transfers) {
            try {
                TenantUtils.execute(transfer.getTenantId(), () -> {
                    if (PayTransferStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus().equals(transfer.getStatus())) {
                        invokeTransfer(transfer.getId());
                        return;
                    }
                    PayChannelDO channel = channelService.getChannel(transfer.getChannelId());
                    if (channel != null) {
                        notifyTransfer(channel, channelService.getPayClient(channel).getTransfer(transfer.getNo()));
                    }
                });
                count++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[syncTransfer] 转账 {} 补查失败", transfer.getNo(), e);
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    // getTransfer、getTransferPage 直接委托 Mapper
}
```
