### Task 12: 钱包充值与充值套餐

**Files:**
- Create: `{pbiz}/dal/dataobject/wallet/{PayWalletRechargePackageDO,PayWalletRechargeDO}.java`、`{pbiz}/dal/mysql/wallet/{PayWalletRechargePackageMapper,PayWalletRechargeMapper}.java`
- Create: `{pbiz}/service/wallet/{PayWalletRechargePackageService,PayWalletRechargePackageServiceImpl,PayWalletRechargeService,PayWalletRechargeServiceImpl}.java`
- Create: `{pbiz}/controller/admin/wallet/{PayWalletRechargePackageController,PayWalletRechargeController}.java` 与 `vo/{PayWalletRechargePackageSaveReqVO,PayWalletRechargePackagePageReqVO,PayWalletRechargePackageRespVO,PayWalletRechargeRespVO}.java`
- Create: `{pbiz}/controller/app/wallet/{AppPayWalletRechargePackageController,AppPayWalletRechargeController}.java` 与 `vo/{AppPayWalletRechargeCreateReqVO,AppPayWalletRechargeCreateRespVO,AppPayWalletRechargePackageRespVO,AppPayWalletRechargeRespVO}.java`
- Test: `{ptest}/service/wallet/PayWalletRechargePackageServiceImplTest.java`、`{ptest}/service/wallet/PayWalletRechargeServiceImplTest.java`、`{ptest}/controller/app/wallet/AppPayWalletRechargeControllerTest.java`、`{ptest}/controller/admin/wallet/PayWalletRechargeControllerTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PayWalletService`（Task 6）、`PayOrderService.createOrder(PayAppDO, …)`（Task 8）、`PayRefundService.createRefund(PayAppDO, …)`（Task 10，外层事务提交后才调用渠道）、`PayAppService.validPayApp(String)`。
- Produces: 共享约定中的 `PayWalletRechargePackageService`、`PayWalletRechargeService`。充值支付单的商户订单号为 `"WR" + 充值编号`，充值退款的商户退款单号为 `"WR" + 充值编号 + "-R"`（Task 13 据此反查充值记录）。

- [ ] **Step 1: DO 与 Mapper**

`PayWalletRechargePackageDO`（`pay_wallet_recharge_package`）：`Long id; String name; Integer payPrice; Integer bonusPrice; Integer status;`

`PayWalletRechargeDO`（`pay_wallet_recharge`）：`Long id; Long walletId; Integer totalPrice; Integer payPrice; Integer bonusPrice; Long packageId; Boolean payStatus; Long payOrderId; String payChannelCode; LocalDateTime payTime; Long payRefundId; Integer refundTotalPrice; Integer refundPayPrice; Integer refundBonusPrice; LocalDateTime refundTime; Integer refundStatus;`

`PayWalletRechargePackageMapper`：`selectByName(String name)`、`selectPage(PayWalletRechargePackagePageReqVO)`（`name` 模糊、`status`）、`selectListByStatus(Integer status)`（按 `payPrice` 升序）。

`PayWalletRechargeMapper`：

```java
    default PageResult<PayWalletRechargeDO> selectPage(Long walletId, PageParam pageParam) {
        return selectPage(pageParam, new LambdaQueryWrapperX<PayWalletRechargeDO>()
                .eqIfPresent(PayWalletRechargeDO::getWalletId, walletId).orderByDesc(PayWalletRechargeDO::getId));
    }

    /** 未支付 → 已支付，防止重复入账 */
    default int updateWhenPaid(Long id, PayWalletRechargeDO update) {
        return update(update, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<PayWalletRechargeDO>().eq(PayWalletRechargeDO::getId, id)
                .eq(PayWalletRechargeDO::getPayStatus, false));
    }

    /** 已支付且未发起过退款 → 退款中（写入退款单编号） */
    default int updateWhenRefunding(Long id, PayWalletRechargeDO update) {
        return update(update, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<PayWalletRechargeDO>().eq(PayWalletRechargeDO::getId, id)
                .eq(PayWalletRechargeDO::getPayStatus, true).isNull(PayWalletRechargeDO::getPayRefundId));
    }

    /** 退款中 → 退款成功或失败 */
    default int updateWhenRefundFinished(Long id, PayWalletRechargeDO update) {
        return update(update, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<PayWalletRechargeDO>().eq(PayWalletRechargeDO::getId, id)
                .isNotNull(PayWalletRechargeDO::getPayRefundId)
                .eq(PayWalletRechargeDO::getRefundStatus, PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus()));
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 写服务测试（RED）**

`PayWalletRechargePackageServiceImplTest`（`BasePayDbUnitTest`）：套餐名重复报 `WALLET_RECHARGE_PACKAGE_NAME_EXISTS`（更新为自己的名字不报错）；`getEnablePackageList` 不含关闭的套餐。

`PayWalletRechargeServiceImplTest`（`BasePayDbAndRedisUnitTest`，`@Import` Task 8 的全部类加 `PayRefundServiceImpl`、`PayWalletServiceImpl`、`PayWalletRechargeServiceImpl`、`PayWalletRechargePackageServiceImpl`；`setUp` 建应用 `wallet`（与 `PayProperties` 默认的充值应用标识一致）及其 mock 渠道、套餐「充 100 送 10」）：

```java
    private static final long MEMBER = 81001L;

    private PayWalletRechargeDO recharge(Long packageId, Integer payPrice) {
        return rechargeService.createRecharge(MEMBER, UserTypeEnum.MEMBER.getValue(),
                new AppPayWalletRechargeCreateReqVO().setPackageId(packageId).setPayPrice(payPrice), "127.0.0.1");
    }

    /** 模拟支付成功消息（Task 13 的消费者会这样调用） */
    private void paid(PayWalletRechargeDO recharge) {
        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectById(recharge.getPayOrderId());
        rechargeService.handleRechargePaid(new PayOrderMessage().setTenantId(TENANT_ID).setAppId(order.getAppId())
                .setAppKey("wallet").setOrderId(order.getId()).setMerchantOrderId(order.getMerchantOrderId())
                .setPrice(order.getPrice()).setChannelCode("mock").setSuccessTime(LocalDateTime.now()));
    }

    @Test
    void 未建充值应用时提示未开通() {
        appMapper.deleteById(rechargeApp.getId());
        assertServiceException(() -> recharge(pkg.getId(), null), WALLET_RECHARGE_NOT_OPENED);
    }

    @Test
    void 按套餐充值_写充值记录并创建会员本人的支付单() {
        PayWalletRechargeDO r = recharge(pkg.getId(), null);
        assertThat(r.getPayPrice()).isEqualTo(10000);
        assertThat(r.getBonusPrice()).isEqualTo(1000);
        assertThat(r.getTotalPrice()).isEqualTo(11000);
        PayOrderDO order = orderMapper.selectById(r.getPayOrderId());
        assertThat(order.getMerchantOrderId()).isEqualTo("WR" + r.getId());
        assertThat(order.getPrice()).isEqualTo(10000);
        assertThat(order.getUserId()).isEqualTo(MEMBER);
    }

    @Test
    void 自定义金额无赠送_既无套餐也无金额报错_套餐关闭报错() {
        assertThat(recharge(null, 500).getBonusPrice()).isZero();
        assertServiceException(() -> recharge(null, null), WALLET_RECHARGE_PRICE_INVALID);
        packageMapper.updateById(new PayWalletRechargePackageDO().setId(pkg.getId()).setStatus(1));
        assertServiceException(() -> recharge(pkg.getId(), null), WALLET_RECHARGE_PACKAGE_NOT_FOUND);
    }

    @Test
    void 支付成功后入账实付加赠送_重复消息只入账一次() {
        PayWalletRechargeDO r = recharge(pkg.getId(), null);
        paid(r);
        paid(r);
        assertThat(walletService.getWallet(r.getWalletId()).getBalance()).isEqualTo(11000);
        assertThat(rechargeMapper.selectById(r.getId()).getPayStatus()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void 充值退款_扣回到账总额_对原支付单退实付金额() {
        PayWalletRechargeDO r = recharge(pkg.getId(), null);
        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, PayOrderResult.success(submit(r), "CH", 10000, LocalDateTime.now(), "{}"));
        paid(r);

        rechargeService.refundRecharge(r.getId(), "127.0.0.1");

        assertThat(walletService.getWallet(r.getWalletId()).getBalance()).isZero();
        PayWalletRechargeDO after = rechargeMapper.selectById(r.getId());
        assertThat(after.getPayRefundId()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(after.getRefundTotalPrice()).isEqualTo(11000);
        PayRefundDO refund = refundMapper.selectById(after.getPayRefundId());
        assertThat(refund.getRefundPrice()).isEqualTo(10000);
        assertThat(refund.getMerchantRefundId()).isEqualTo("WR" + r.getId() + "-R");
        assertServiceException(() -> rechargeService.refundRecharge(r.getId(), "127.0.0.1"), WALLET_RECHARGE_REFUND_STATUS_ERROR);
    }

    @Test
    void 余额已被花掉时不能充值退款_未支付的充值不能退款() {
        PayWalletRechargeDO r = recharge(pkg.getId(), null);
        assertServiceException(() -> rechargeService.refundRecharge(r.getId(), "127.0.0.1"), WALLET_RECHARGE_REFUND_STATUS_ERROR);
        orderService.notifyOrder(channel, PayOrderResult.success(submit(r), "CH", 10000, LocalDateTime.now(), "{}"));
        paid(r);
        walletService.reduceBalance(r.getWalletId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.PAYMENT, "P-spent", "消费", 5000);
        assertServiceException(() -> rechargeService.refundRecharge(r.getId(), "127.0.0.1"), WALLET_BALANCE_NOT_ENOUGH);
        assertThat(rechargeMapper.selectById(r.getId()).getPayRefundId()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void 退款成功置为退款成功_退款失败退回扣除的金额() {
        PayWalletRechargeDO ok = paidAndRefunding();     // 本类辅助方法：充值、支付、入账、发起充值退款，返回最新记录
        rechargeService.handleRechargeRefunded(refundMessage(ok, PayRefundStatusEnum.SUCCESS));
        assertThat(rechargeMapper.selectById(ok.getId()).getRefundStatus()).isEqualTo(PayRefundStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus());

        PayWalletRechargeDO fail = paidAndRefunding();
        int before = walletService.getWallet(fail.getWalletId()).getBalance();
        rechargeService.handleRechargeRefunded(refundMessage(fail, PayRefundStatusEnum.FAILURE));
        rechargeService.handleRechargeRefunded(refundMessage(fail, PayRefundStatusEnum.FAILURE));
        assertThat(rechargeMapper.selectById(fail.getId()).getRefundStatus()).isEqualTo(PayRefundStatusEnum.FAILURE.getStatus());
        assertThat(walletService.getWallet(fail.getWalletId()).getBalance()).as("扣回的 11000 退回且只退一次")
                .isEqualTo(before + 11000);
    }
```

（`submit(r)`：对充值支付单提交 mock 渠道并返回支付尝试的外部单号；`refundMessage(r, status)`：按退款单组装 `PayRefundMessage`，`merchantRefundId` 为 `"WR" + id + "-R"`。）

Run: `mvn -o -q -pl ccloud-module-pay/ccloud-module-pay-biz -am test -Dtest='PayWalletRecharge*Test' -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
Expected: FAIL（编译失败）。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`PayWalletRechargePackageServiceImpl`：增删改查，`validatePackageNameUnique(Long id, String name)`；`getEnablePackageList` 取 `status = 0`。VO：`PayWalletRechargePackageSaveReqVO`（`Long id; @NotBlank @Size(max = 32) String name; @NotNull @Min(1) Integer payPrice; @NotNull @Min(0) Integer bonusPrice; @NotNull @InEnum(CommonStatusEnum.class) Integer status;`）。

`PayWalletRechargeServiceImpl` 要点：

```java
    private static final String MERCHANT_ORDER_PREFIX = "WR";
    private static final String MERCHANT_REFUND_SUFFIX = "-R";

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public PayWalletRechargeDO createRecharge(Long userId, Integer userType, AppPayWalletRechargeCreateReqVO reqVO, String userIp) {
        PayAppDO app = getRechargeApp();
        int payPrice;
        int bonusPrice;
        if (reqVO.getPackageId() != null) {
            PayWalletRechargePackageDO pkg = packageService.getPackage(reqVO.getPackageId());
            if (pkg == null || !CommonStatusEnum.ENABLE.getStatus().equals(pkg.getStatus())) {
                throw exception(WALLET_RECHARGE_PACKAGE_NOT_FOUND);
            }
            payPrice = pkg.getPayPrice();
            bonusPrice = pkg.getBonusPrice();
        } else if (reqVO.getPayPrice() != null && reqVO.getPayPrice() > 0) {
            payPrice = reqVO.getPayPrice();
            bonusPrice = 0;
        } else {
            throw exception(WALLET_RECHARGE_PRICE_INVALID);
        }
        PayWalletDO wallet = walletService.getOrCreateWallet(userId, userType);
        PayWalletRechargeDO recharge = new PayWalletRechargeDO().setWalletId(wallet.getId()).setPackageId(reqVO.getPackageId())
                .setPayPrice(payPrice).setBonusPrice(bonusPrice).setTotalPrice(payPrice + bonusPrice).setPayStatus(false)
                .setRefundStatus(PayRefundStatusEnum.WAITING.getStatus())
                .setRefundTotalPrice(0).setRefundPayPrice(0).setRefundBonusPrice(0);
        rechargeMapper.insert(recharge);
        Long orderId = orderService.createOrder(app, new PayOrderCreateReqDTO().setAppKey(app.getAppKey())
                .setMerchantOrderId(MERCHANT_ORDER_PREFIX + recharge.getId()).setSubject("钱包充值")
                .setBody("充值 " + MoneyUtils.fenToYuanStr(payPrice) + " 元").setPrice(payPrice)
                .setExpireTime(LocalDateTime.now().plus(payProperties.getWallet().getRechargeExpire()))
                .setUserIp(userIp).setUserId(userId).setUserType(userType));
        rechargeMapper.updateById(new PayWalletRechargeDO().setId(recharge.getId()).setPayOrderId(orderId));
        return recharge.setPayOrderId(orderId);
    }

    private PayAppDO getRechargeApp() {
        try {
            return appService.validPayApp(payProperties.getWallet().getRechargeAppKey());
        } catch (ServiceException e) {
            throw exception(WALLET_RECHARGE_NOT_OPENED);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRechargeApp(Long appId) {
        PayAppDO app = appService.getApp(appId);
        return app != null && app.getAppKey().equals(payProperties.getWallet().getRechargeAppKey());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void handleRechargePaid(PayOrderMessage message) {
        PayWalletRechargeDO recharge = rechargeMapper.selectById(parseId(message.getMerchantOrderId()));
        if (recharge == null || Boolean.TRUE.equals(recharge.getPayStatus())) {
            return;
        }
        if (!recharge.getPayPrice().equals(message.getPrice())) {
            log.error("[handleRechargePaid] 充值 {} 实付 {} 与支付金额 {} 不一致，不入账", recharge.getId(), recharge.getPayPrice(), message.getPrice());
            return;
        }
        if (rechargeMapper.updateWhenPaid(recharge.getId(), new PayWalletRechargeDO().setPayStatus(true)
                .setPayOrderId(message.getOrderId()).setPayChannelCode(message.getChannelCode())
                .setPayTime(message.getSuccessTime())) == 0) {
            return;
        }
        walletService.addBalance(recharge.getWalletId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.RECHARGE, String.valueOf(recharge.getId()),
                "充值", recharge.getTotalPrice());
    }

    /**
     * 先扣回到账总额（实付加赠送），再对原支付单退实付金额。退款渠道调用推迟到本事务提交之后；
     * 退款失败时由 handleRechargeRefunded 把扣回的金额退回
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void refundRecharge(Long id, String userIp) {
        PayWalletRechargeDO recharge = rechargeMapper.selectById(id);
        if (recharge == null) {
            throw exception(WALLET_RECHARGE_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(recharge.getPayStatus()) || recharge.getPayRefundId() != null) {
            throw exception(WALLET_RECHARGE_REFUND_STATUS_ERROR);
        }
        walletService.reduceBalance(recharge.getWalletId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.RECHARGE_REFUND, String.valueOf(id),
                "充值退款", recharge.getTotalPrice());
        PayAppDO app = getRechargeApp();
        Long refundId = refundService.createRefund(app, new PayRefundCreateReqDTO().setAppKey(app.getAppKey())
                .setMerchantOrderId(MERCHANT_ORDER_PREFIX + id).setMerchantRefundId(MERCHANT_ORDER_PREFIX + id + MERCHANT_REFUND_SUFFIX)
                .setPrice(recharge.getPayPrice()).setReason("钱包充值退款").setUserIp(userIp));
        if (rechargeMapper.updateWhenRefunding(id, new PayWalletRechargeDO().setPayRefundId(refundId)
                .setRefundTotalPrice(recharge.getTotalPrice()).setRefundPayPrice(recharge.getPayPrice())
                .setRefundBonusPrice(recharge.getBonusPrice())) == 0) {
            throw exception(WALLET_RECHARGE_REFUND_STATUS_ERROR);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void handleRechargeRefunded(PayRefundMessage message) {
        String merchantRefundId = message.getMerchantRefundId();
        if (!StrUtil.endWith(merchantRefundId, MERCHANT_REFUND_SUFFIX)) {
            return;
        }
        PayWalletRechargeDO recharge = rechargeMapper.selectById(parseId(StrUtil.removeSuffix(merchantRefundId, MERCHANT_REFUND_SUFFIX)));
        if (recharge == null) {
            return;
        }
        boolean success = PayRefundStatusEnum.SUCCESS.getStatus().equals(message.getStatus());
        int updated = rechargeMapper.updateWhenRefundFinished(recharge.getId(), new PayWalletRechargeDO()
                .setRefundStatus(message.getStatus()).setRefundTime(success ? message.getSuccessTime() : null));
        if (updated == 0 || success) {
            return;
        }
        // 退款失败：退回发起时扣回的金额。记为充值类型，使累计充值随之恢复
        walletService.addBalance(recharge.getWalletId(), PayWalletBizTypeEnum.RECHARGE,
                recharge.getId() + "-refund-rollback", "充值退款失败退回", recharge.getRefundTotalPrice());
    }

    private static Long parseId(String merchantId) {
        return Long.valueOf(StrUtil.removePrefix(merchantId, MERCHANT_ORDER_PREFIX));
    }
```

`getRechargePage(walletId, pageParam)` 委托 Mapper。

```
pay.transfer.success / pay.transfer.failure：merchantTransferId、transferNo、price、status、successTime、errorMsg
```

```java
class PayOpenNotifyServiceImplTest extends BaseMockitoUnitTest {

    @InjectMocks
    private PayOpenNotifyServiceImpl service;
    @Mock
    private PayAppService appService;
    @Mock
    private OpenNotifyApi openNotifyApi;

    @Test
    void 重投时以消息编号作为事件编号_发给绑定的开放应用() {
        when(appService.getAppIgnoreTenant(1L)).thenReturn(new PayAppDO().setId(1L).setOpenAppId(9L));
        when(openNotifyApi.sendNotify(any())).thenReturn(CommonResult.success(100L));
        service.forward("msg-001", "pay.order.paid", 1L, Map.of("merchantOrderId", "M1"));

        ArgumentCaptor<OpenNotifySendReqDTO> captor = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(OpenNotifySendReqDTO.class);
        verify(openNotifyApi).sendNotify(captor.capture());
        assertThat(captor.getValue().getAppId()).isEqualTo(9L);
        assertThat(captor.getValue().getEventId()).isEqualTo("msg-001");
        assertThat(captor.getValue().getEvent()).isEqualTo("pay.order.paid");
    }

    @Test
    void 未绑定开放应用时不发送() {
        when(appService.getAppIgnoreTenant(1L)).thenReturn(new PayAppDO().setId(1L));
        service.forward("msg-002", "pay.order.paid", 1L, Map.of());
        verifyNoInteractions(openNotifyApi);
    }

    @Test
    void 开放平台调用失败时抛出_让消息重投() {
        when(appService.getAppIgnoreTenant(1L)).thenReturn(new PayAppDO().setId(1L).setOpenAppId(9L));
        when(openNotifyApi.sendNotify(any())).thenReturn(CommonResult.error(500, "down"));
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.forward("msg-003", "pay.order.paid", 1L, Map.of())).isInstanceOf(ServiceException.class);
    }
}
```

```java
        });
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 实现**

```java
@Service
@Slf4j
public class PayOpenNotifyServiceImpl implements PayOpenNotifyService {

    @Resource
    private PayAppService appService;
    @Resource
    private OpenNotifyApi openNotifyApi;

    /**
     * 以消息编号作为事件编号：消费事务提交失败导致消息重投时，开放平台按事件编号去重，第三方只收到一次
     */
    @Override
    public void forward(String messageId, String event, Long appId, Map<String, Object> data) {
        PayAppDO app = appService.getAppIgnoreTenant(appId);
        if (app == null || app.getOpenAppId() == null) {
            return;
        }
        openNotifyApi.sendNotify(new OpenNotifySendReqDTO().setAppId(app.getOpenAppId()).setEventId(messageId)
                .setEvent(event).setData(data)).getCheckedData();
    }
}
```

```java
/**
 * 支付成功：钱包充值订单入账；绑定了开放应用的支付应用转发给外部系统。两件事各自幂等，
 * 其中一件失败时消息重投，已完成的那件会被跳过
 */
@Component
public class PayOrderPaidListener extends AbstractRedisStreamMessageListener<PayOrderMessage> {

    static final String CONSUMER_RECHARGE = "pay-wallet-recharge";
    static final String CONSUMER_OPEN_NOTIFY = "pay-open-notify";

    @Resource
    private IdempotentConsumer idempotentConsumer;
    @Resource
    private PayWalletRechargeService rechargeService;
    @Resource
    private PayOpenNotifyService openNotifyService;

    public PayOrderPaidListener() {
        super(PayOrderMessage.TOPIC_PAID, PayOrderMessage.class);
    }

    @Override
    public void onMessage(String messageId, PayOrderMessage message) {
        TenantUtils.execute(message.getTenantId(), () -> {
            if (rechargeService.isRechargeApp(message.getAppId())) {
                idempotentConsumer.consumeOnce(messageId, CONSUMER_RECHARGE, () -> rechargeService.handleRechargePaid(message));
            }
            Map<String, Object> data = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            data.put("merchantOrderId", message.getMerchantOrderId());
            data.put("orderNo", message.getOrderNo());
            data.put("price", message.getPrice());
            data.put("status", "SUCCESS");
            data.put("successTime", toMillis(message.getSuccessTime()));
            data.put("channelCode", message.getChannelCode());
            idempotentConsumer.consumeOnce(messageId, CONSUMER_OPEN_NOTIFY,
                    () -> openNotifyService.forward(messageId, OpenNotifyEvents.PAY_ORDER_PAID, message.getAppId(), data));
        });
    }

    static Long toMillis(LocalDateTime time) {
        return time == null ? null : LocalDateTimeUtil.toEpochMilli(time);
    }
}
```

`AbstractPayRefundListener`：构造参数为主题与开放平台事件名；`onMessage` 在消息租户下，充值应用先 `consumeOnce(messageId, "pay-wallet-recharge", () -> rechargeService.handleRechargeRefunded(message))`，再按上面的 `data` 约定转发（`status` 为 `SUCCESS` 或 `FAILURE`，由 `message.getStatus()` 映射）。`PayRefundSuccessListener` / `PayRefundFailureListener` 只提供构造参数。`AbstractPayTransferListener` 与两个子类同理，只转发。

```java
    private MockHttpServletRequestBuilder json(MockHttpServletRequestBuilder builder, String body) {
        return builder.contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content(body);
    }

    @Test
    void 开放应用创建并查询支付单_重复创建返回同一单() throws Exception {
        String body = "{\"merchantOrderId\":\"WT-O1\",\"subject\":\"开放下单\",\"price\":100,\"expireTime\":"
                + LocalDateTimeUtil.toEpochMilli(LocalDateTime.now().plusHours(1)) + "}";
        mockMvc.perform(asOpenApp(json(post("/open-api/pay/order/create"), body), 9001L))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(0)).andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.status").value(0));
        mockMvc.perform(asOpenApp(json(post("/open-api/pay/order/create"), body), 9001L))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(0));
        mockMvc.perform(asOpenApp(get("/open-api/pay/order/get").param("merchantOrderId", "WT-O1"), 9001L))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.merchantOrderId").value("WT-O1"));
        Integer count = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pay_order WHERE merchant_order_id = 'WT-O1'", Integer.class);
        assertThat(count).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void 提交支付不能选钱包_未绑定的开放应用不能使用支付() throws Exception {
        // 先建 WT-O2，再以 wallet 提交
        mockMvc.perform(asOpenApp(json(post("/open-api/pay/order/submit"), "{\"merchantOrderId\":\"WT-O2\",\"channelCode\":\"wallet\"}"), 9001L))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(1_007_002_005));
        mockMvc.perform(asOpenApp(get("/open-api/pay/order/get").param("merchantOrderId", "WT-O2"), 9999L))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(1_007_000_006));
    }

    @Test
    void 只能查本应用的订单() throws Exception {
        TenantUtils.execute(TENANT, () -> {
            PayAppDO other = new PayAppDO().setAppKey("webtest-open2").setName("另一个应用").setStatus(0).setOpenAppId(9002L);
            appMapper.insert(other);
            orderService.createOrder(other, new PayOrderCreateReqDTO().setAppKey("webtest-open2").setMerchantOrderId("WT-O3")
                    .setSubject("他人的单").setPrice(100).setExpireTime(LocalDateTime.now().plusHours(1)).setUserIp("127.0.0.1"));
        });
        mockMvc.perform(asOpenApp(get("/open-api/pay/order/get").param("merchantOrderId", "WT-O3"), 9001L))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(1_007_002_000));
        mockMvc.perform(asOpenApp(get("/open-api/pay/order/get").param("merchantOrderId", "WT-O3"), 9002L))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(0));
    }

    @Test
    void 管理员或会员身份访问开放接口返回401() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(asAdmin(get("/open-api/pay/order/get").param("merchantOrderId", "x"), ADMIN_ID))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(401));
    }
```

（`expireTime` 的 JSON 格式以框架的 Jackson 配置为准：项目对日期使用毫秒时间戳，与 `OpenMemberUserController` 返回值一致。提交 `wallet` 前须插入一条启用的 `wallet` 渠道，否则先报渠道不存在。）

`OpenPayRefundControllerTest`：`setUp` 中经服务创建、提交、`notifyOrder` 成功一笔 `WT-R1`（100 分）；`POST /open-api/pay/refund/create`（`merchantRefundId` `WT-RF1`、`price` 30）返回成功；`GET /open-api/pay/refund/get?merchantRefundId=WT-RF1` 返回 `refundPrice` 30。

Run: `mvn -o -q -pl ccloud-module-pay/ccloud-module-pay-biz -am test -Dtest='AppPayOrderControllerTest,AppPayChannelControllerTest,OpenPay*Test' -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
Expected: FAIL。

- [ ] **Step 2: 实现**

`AppPayOrderController`（`/app-api/pay/order`）：

```java
    @GetMapping("/get")
    @Operation(summary = "获得本人的支付单")
    public CommonResult<AppPayOrderRespVO> getOrder(@RequestParam("id") Long id) {
        return success(BeanUtils.toBean(validateOwnOrder(id), AppPayOrderRespVO.class));
    }

    @PostMapping("/submit")
    @Operation(summary = "提交支付", description = "选择渠道发起支付，返回二维码、跳转链接等展示信息；钱包支付直接返回结果")
    public CommonResult<PayOrderSubmitRespVO> submitOrder(@Valid @RequestBody AppPayOrderSubmitReqVO reqVO,
                                                          HttpServletRequest request) {
        validateOwnOrder(reqVO.getId());
        return success(orderService.submitOrder(reqVO.getId(), reqVO, ServletUtils.getClientIP(request)));
    }

    /**
     * 只能操作付款人是自己的订单；不存在与不属于自己统一报"支付单不存在"，不泄露订单是否存在
     */
    private PayOrderDO validateOwnOrder(Long id) {
        PayOrderDO order = orderService.getOrder(id);
        if (order == null || !Objects.equals(order.getUserId(), getLoginUserId())
                || !UserTypeEnum.MEMBER.getValue().equals(order.getUserType())) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return order;
    }
```

`AppPayOrderSubmitReqVO extends PayOrderSubmitReqVO`，加 `@NotNull Long id;`。`AppPayOrderRespVO`：`Long id; Long appId; String subject; String body; Integer price; Integer status; LocalDateTime expireTime; LocalDateTime successTime;`。

`AppPayChannelController`（`/app-api/pay/channel`）：`GET /list?appId=` 返回 `getEnableChannelList(appId)` 映射为 `AppPayChannelRespVO`（`String code; String name;`）。

`OpenPayOrderController`（`/open-api/pay/order`；调用身份为网关下发的开放应用，`getLoginUserId()` 即开放应用编号）：

```java
    @PostMapping("/create")
    @Operation(summary = "创建支付单", description = "按商户订单号幂等：重复创建返回原单，金额不同报错")
    public CommonResult<OpenPayOrderRespVO> createOrder(@Valid @RequestBody OpenPayOrderCreateReqVO reqVO, HttpServletRequest request) {
        PayAppDO app = appService.validPayAppByOpenAppId(getLoginUserId());
        Long id = orderService.createOrder(app, new PayOrderCreateReqDTO().setAppKey(app.getAppKey())
                .setMerchantOrderId(reqVO.getMerchantOrderId()).setSubject(reqVO.getSubject()).setBody(reqVO.getBody())
                .setPrice(reqVO.getPrice()).setExpireTime(reqVO.getExpireTime()).setUserIp(ServletUtils.getClientIP(request)));
        return success(toResp(orderService.getOrder(id)));
    }

    @GetMapping("/get")
    @Operation(summary = "按商户订单号查询支付单")
    public CommonResult<OpenPayOrderRespVO> getOrder(@RequestParam("merchantOrderId") String merchantOrderId) {
        return success(toResp(validateAppOrder(merchantOrderId)));
    }

    @PostMapping("/submit")
    @Operation(summary = "提交支付", description = "外部系统的订单没有付款会员，不能选择钱包")
    public CommonResult<PayOrderSubmitRespVO> submitOrder(@Valid @RequestBody OpenPayOrderSubmitReqVO reqVO, HttpServletRequest request) {
        PayOrderDO order = validateAppOrder(reqVO.getMerchantOrderId());
        return success(orderService.submitOrder(order.getId(), reqVO, ServletUtils.getClientIP(request)));
    }

    private PayOrderDO validateAppOrder(String merchantOrderId) {
        PayAppDO app = appService.validPayAppByOpenAppId(getLoginUserId());
        PayOrderDO order = orderService.getOrder(app.getId(), merchantOrderId);
        if (order == null) {
            throw exception(PAY_ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return order;
    }
```

VO：`OpenPayOrderCreateReqVO`（`@NotBlank @Size(max = 64) String merchantOrderId; @NotBlank @Size(max = 32) String subject; @Size(max = 128) String body; @NotNull @Min(1) Integer price; @NotNull @Future LocalDateTime expireTime;`）、`OpenPayOrderSubmitReqVO extends PayOrderSubmitReqVO`（加 `@NotBlank String merchantOrderId;`）、`OpenPayOrderRespVO`（`String merchantOrderId; String no; String subject; Integer price; Integer status; Integer refundPrice; String channelCode; String payMethod; LocalDateTime expireTime; LocalDateTime successTime;`）。外部系统用商户单号定位订单，响应中不返回内部编号。

`OpenPayRefundController`（`/open-api/pay/refund`）：`POST /create`（`OpenPayRefundCreateReqVO`：`@NotBlank String merchantOrderId; @NotBlank @Size(max = 64) String merchantRefundId; @NotNull @Min(1) Integer price; @NotBlank @Size(max = 256) String reason;`）调 `refundService.createRefund(app, dto)` 后返回 `OpenPayRefundRespVO`（`merchantOrderId, merchantRefundId, refundNo, refundPrice, status, successTime, channelErrorMsg`）；`GET /get?merchantRefundId=` 按 `(app.id, merchantRefundId)` 查询，不存在 `PAY_REFUND_NOT_FOUND`。

```java
class PayJobsTest extends BaseMockitoUnitTest {

    @Mock
    private PayOrderService orderService;
    @Mock
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 实现**

```java
/**
 * 关闭超时未支付的订单（先向渠道确认未支付），需在 XXL-Job 为本服务登记（每分钟执行）
 */
@Component
public class PayOrderExpireJob {

    @Resource
    private PayOrderService orderService;
    @Resource
    private PayProperties payProperties;

    @XxlJob("payOrderExpireJob")
    public void execute() {
        int count = orderService.expireOrder(payProperties.getJob().getBatchSize());
        XxlJobHelper.handleSuccess(String.format("关闭超时订单 %d 笔", count));
    }
}
```

`PayOrderSyncJob`（`payOrderSyncJob`，"补查等待中的支付尝试 %d 笔"）、`PayRefundSyncJob`（`payRefundSyncJob`）、`PayTransferSyncJob`（`payTransferSyncJob`）同构。各任务都不加 `@TenantJob`：服务方法内部跨租户查询、逐条切换到单据所属租户处理。`MqJobHandler` 从 `{obiz}/job/mq/MqJobHandler.java` 复制并替换包名，注释改为"pay 消费组（支付、退款、转账消息）的未确认消息重投"。

**Interfaces:**

- [ ] **Step 1: 编排**

`docker/.env` 在 `OPEN_PORT=28084` 之后加 `PAY_PORT=28085`。`docker-compose.yml` 照 `ccloud-open` 复制一段 `ccloud-pay`：`build: ../ccloud-module-pay/ccloud-module-pay-biz`，`image: ccloud/ccloud-pay-server:1.0.0`，`container_name: ccloud-pay`，`ports: "127.0.0.1:${PAY_PORT}:28085"`，健康检查端口 28085，`depends_on` 同 open（system 健康）。`grep -n "OPEN_PORT" scripts docker -r` 找出读取端口的其他位置并同样加上 `PAY_PORT`。

- [ ] **Step 2: Nacos 配置包**

`docker/nacos/config/ccloud-pay-server.yaml`：

```yaml
# ccloud-pay-server 的可调配置
#
# 模拟支付开关 mock-enabled 与渠道回调地址前缀 notify-base-url 不在这里：前者只能由 local / dev 的 profile 打开
# （写进配置包会覆盖各环境的 profile，让生产也能用模拟支付白拿商品），后者属于各环境的部署地址

ccloud:
  pay:
    wallet:
      # 钱包充值所用支付应用的标识，各租户在后台自建同名支付应用并开通渠道
      recharge-app-key: wallet
      # 充值支付单的有效期
      recharge-expire: 2h
    job:
      # 定时任务每次处理的条数上限
      batch-size: 100
      # 支付尝试创建多久后仍在等待才主动查单
      sync-delay: 1m
      # 只补查这段时间内创建的支付尝试
      sync-window: 1d
```

`docker/nacos/README.md` 的配置包说明表加一行 `| ccloud-pay-server.yaml | pay | 钱包充值应用标识、充值单有效期、定时任务批量与补查时间（模拟支付开关与回调地址不在配置包中，见约定 6） |`，并新增约定 6：模拟支付开关 `ccloud.pay.mock-enabled` 只在 local、dev 的 profile 中为 true，配置包不得包含（由 `verify-nacos.sh` 检查）；生产必须为 false，否则任何人都能经免登录的确认页让 Mock 订单"支付成功"。

`scripts/verify-nacos.sh` 在 `allow-private-address` 检查之后加同样写法的检查：

```bash
mock_on=$(grep -HnEi '^[[:space:]]*mock-enabled[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*true([[:space:]]|#|$)' "$CONFIG_DIR"/*.yaml || true)
[ -z "$mock_on" ] || fail "配置包不得出现 mock-enabled: true（模拟支付开关只在 profile 中设置）：
$mock_on"
```

用临时目录自测：复制配置包到临时目录、在其中一个文件加 `mock-enabled: true`，`NACOS_CONFIG_DIR=<临时目录> scripts/verify-nacos.sh --check-only` 应失败；对仓库原目录应通过。


### Task 17: 端到端验收与文档

**Files:**

- Modify: `README.md`、`docs/快速开始.md`、`docs/模块说明.md`、`docs/接口鉴权说明.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 全部前序任务；开放平台一段创建的 `OPEN_APP_ID`、`APP_KEY`、`NEW_SECRET`、回调接收方 `RECV_URL`、`$OTMP/last.json`。

- [ ] **Step 1: 端到端脚本**

在 `scripts/verify-e2e.sh` 的"开放平台"一段之后、"文档、链路追踪…"之前加 `echo "==> 支付"` 一段；`trap` 改为 `'pay_cleanup; open_cleanup; infra_cleanup'`。首行注释加"+ P3 支付"。内容：

1. **准备**：记下演示会员钱包余额 `BAL0`（`lq "SELECT balance FROM ccloud.pay_wallet WHERE user_id=$MEMBER_ID AND user_type=1"`，无则 0）与 `RECHARGE0`（`SELECT COALESCE(MAX(id),0) FROM ccloud.pay_wallet_recharge`）。`pay_cleanup` 负责：删除本段创建的支付应用（`app_key LIKE 'e2e-pay-%'`）及其渠道、订单、支付尝试、退款；删除 `mall` 应用下 `merchant_order_id LIKE 'E2E-%'` 的订单、支付尝试与退款；删除 `id > RECHARGE0` 的充值记录及其订单；删除本段产生的钱包流水（按 `create_time >= 本段开始时间` 与演示会员钱包编号）并把余额恢复为 `BAL0`。
2. **外部系统**：用管理员令牌创建支付应用 `e2e-pay-<时间戳>`（`openAppKey` 为开放平台一段新建应用的 `APP_KEY`）与 mock 渠道（配置 `{"notifySecret":"e2e-mock-notify-secret-01","behavior":"SUCCESS","delaySeconds":2}`）；给开放应用授权 `member.user.get` 与 5 个 `pay.*` 接口（授权是整体替换，须一起提交）。新增带请求体的签名调用函数 `open_post`（签名串最后一段为请求体的 SHA-256，其余与 `open_call` 相同），依次：创建支付单 `E2E-P<时间戳>`（100 分）→ 以 `mock` 提交，取 `displayContent` → `curl -X POST "$GW$displayContent"` 确认 → 30 秒内轮询 `GET /open-api/pay/order/get` 直到 `status` 为 10 → 30 秒内 `$OTMP/last.json` 的 `event` 为 `pay.order.paid` 且 `data.merchantOrderId` 一致，并用 `NEW_SECRET` 验签（复用开放平台一段的 Python 验签片段）。`ok "外部系统经开放平台下单、Mock 支付、收到支付成功回调"`。
3. **退款**：`open_post /open-api/pay/refund/create`（30 分）→ 30 秒内查询退款为 10，且回调接收方收到 `pay.refund.success`。`ok "外部系统经开放平台退款并收到回调"`。
4. **会员充值**：`MT` 令牌调 `POST /app-api/pay/wallet-recharge/create`（`{"packageId":1}`）取 `payOrderId` → `POST /app-api/pay/order/submit`（`mock`）→ 确认 → 60 秒内 `GET /app-api/pay/wallet/get` 的余额等于 `BAL0 + 11000`。`ok "会员充值经 Mock 付款后到账（含赠送）"`。
5. **钱包支付与退款**：直连 pay 服务的内部接口（`PS="http://127.0.0.1:${PAY_PORT}"`，带 `$TENANT` 头）`POST $PS/rpc-api/pay/order/create` 为演示会员在 `mall` 应用下建 `E2E-W<时间戳>`（500 分）→ 会员以 `wallet` 提交，返回 `status` 为 10、`displayMode` 为 `NONE` → 余额减少 500 → `POST $PS/rpc-api/pay/refund/create` 退 500 → 30 秒内余额恢复。`ok "钱包余额支付即时成功，退款退回余额"`。经网关访问 `/rpc-api/pay/order/create` 返回 403。
6. **丢失回调由补查补回**：把第 2 步的渠道改为 `DROP_NOTIFY`（`PUT /admin-api/pay/channel/update`，`notifySecret` 传 `***` 保留原值）→ 新建并提交、确认一笔 `E2E-D<时间戳>` → 180 秒内轮询到 `status` 为 10（补查任务每分钟执行，等待 1 分钟后才查单）。`ok "Mock 丢弃回调时由补查任务补回支付结果"`。
7. **生产保护**：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "$GW/app-api/pay/notify/order/999999999" -X POST` 返回 404；用错误签名回调第 2 步的渠道返回 400。

（每一步失败都用 `die` 给出响应内容。Mock 回调从 pay 容器经 `http://ccloud-gateway:28080` 到达网关，由 `application-dev.yaml` 的 `notify-base-url` 决定。）

- [ ] **Step 2: 运行端到端验收**

先确认中间件在运行（`docker ps` 中有 MySQL、Redis、Nacos、XXL-Job、Zipkin）；不在运行时停下，由控制方征得用户同意后启动。然后：

Run: `export PATH=$PATH:/Users/yifei/maven/apache-maven-3.9.9/bin && scripts/docker-up.sh && scripts/verify-e2e.sh && scripts/verify-docker.sh --full`
Expected: 全部 OK，最后输出"端到端验收全部通过"。（`docker-up.sh` 不加 `--reset-db`：本期的表结构变更须重建本机 `ccloud` 库才能生效，由控制方先征得用户同意后执行一次 `scripts/docker-up.sh --reset-db`。）

- `docs/快速开始.md`：执行器、打包列表、IDE 启动类与端口表、jar 列表、"必须配置同一个值"的服务清单、业务服务端口清单都加 pay；生产部署说明加"`ccloud.pay.mock-enabled` 必须为 false；演示数据中的 Mock 渠道须删除"。
- `docs/模块说明.md`：新增 pay 一节：职责、表、渠道层结构、单据状态、消息主题与外部回调事件、定时任务、配置项；指向设计文档。

- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-07-ccloud-design.md`：第 6 节追加"2026-10-12 调整"：支付不再自建回调表，内部服务经 outbox 消息、外部系统经开放平台回调；第 11 节 P3 行注明拆为支付、工作流两个子项目，支付已交付。
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-pay-design.md`：状态行改为"已实现并通过端到端验收"；新增"实现与设计的差异"一节，至少写明：渠道工厂每次取用时比对库中配置、变化即重建，不需要 Redis 发布订阅；钱包支付的扣款与支付尝试置成功分属两个事务，扣款流水即支付凭证，补查按流水判定；开放平台回调增加调用方事件编号去重（以 outbox 消息编号为事件编号）；钱包渠道查不到退款或转账流水时返回等待，由补查按单号幂等重发。

Run: `scripts/verify-docs.sh`
Expected: 通过。
